viṭṭu akalvu eṉṟatu, patapporuḷait tokainilai, payaṉilai mutaliyavaṟṟāṟpakuttu, iḵtu iṉṉa vēṟṟumait toṭar, iḵtu iṉṉa payaṉilaittoṭar eṉṟāṟ pōlac cūttirac coṟporuḷai virittuk kūṟum muṟaiyil amainta akalavuraiyiṉai, avvuraiyiṉ iyalpu aṭutta nūṟpāvil viritturaikkappaṭum. iṅkuk kūṟa eṭuttukkoṇṭa akalavurai pōṉṟu poruḷtāṉē virintu tōṉṟumpaṭi toṭarp poruḷaip pakutturaikkum taṉmaiyatu eṉpār, (viṭṭu akalvu iṉṟi virivoṭu porunti eṉṟār.) viṭutal --- patapporuḷaip piritturaittal. itu kaṇṇaḻittal eṉavum vaḻaṅkappaṭum. akalvu---poruḷ akalamuṭaiyatāy virintamaital. viṭṭakalvu iṉṟi eṉavē, cūttiram kūṟum poruḷait tokuttukkūṟi muṭippatallatu cūttirattiluḷḷa collārāycciyum eḻuttārāycciyum mutalāka aṟiya vēṇṭuvaṉa vellām ikkāṇṭikaiyil iṭam peṟā eṉṟārāyiṟṟu.
iṅkuk kāṇṭikai eṉṟatu, cūttirattu uṭporuḷaik kaṟpōr eḷitiṟ kaṇṭuṇarum muṟaiyil varaiyappaṭum poḻippurai itu, piṇṭap poḻippum kaṇṇaḻitturaikkum poḻippum eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ piṇṭap poḻip pāvatu cūttirapporuḷait tokuttu varaivatu, kaṇṇaḻit turaikkum poḻippāvatu cūttirap poruḷait tokuttu varaivatu, kaṇṇaḻitturaikkum poḻippāvatu poruḷ niṟkum coṟṟoṭaraip poruḷ vakaiyāṟ pakutteṭuttup poruḷ kūṟuvatu itaṉai vārttikam eṉpar vaṭanūlār.
meykaṇṭār tām iyaṟṟiya civañāṉa pōtattiṟku vārttikam eṉṉum ippoḻip puraiyiṉai varaintuḷḷamai iṅkuk kuṟippiṭattakuvatākum. iṅṅaṉam (piṇṭap poḻippum vārttikap poḻippum eṉap) poḻippurai iruvakaippaṭum eṉpatu,
		poḻippeṉap paṭuvatu poruntiya poruḷaip
piṇṭa mākka koṇṭuraip patuvē
eṉavum,
		pāṭaṅ kaṇṇaḻivu kāraṇa meṉṟivai
nāṭiṟ ṟiripila vākutal poḻippē
eṉavum, ōtiyavāṟṟāṉ aṟika (civañāṉa pāṭiyam cū-1) eṉac civañāṉa muṉivar tarum viḷakkam, tolkāppiyam marapiyal 103, 104 -ām cūttiraṅkaḷukkup pērāciriyar varain tuḷḷa uraippakutiyait taḻuviyamaintatākum.	(104)
105.	cūttirat tuṭporu ḷaṉṟiyum yāppuṟa
iṉṟi yamaiyā tiyaipavai ellām
oṉṟa uraippa turaiyeṉap paṭumē.
iḷampūraṇam :
uraiyāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ) cūttirattuṭ poruḷoḻiyavum annūlakattil yāppiṟkum porunta iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉa vellāṅ koṇarntu porunta uraippatu uraiyāku meṉṟavāṟu.
pērāciriyam :
itu mēl,
		otta cūttira muraippiṟ kāṇṭikai
meyppaṭak kiḷarnta vakaiya tāki	(tol-mara: 98)
eṉa niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē kāṇṭikaiyiṉai meyppaṭak kiḷantavakai yuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ) mēl,
paḻippil cūttiram paṭṭa paṇpiṉ	(tol-mara: 101)
varutalum, atuvē ētuvum eṭuttukkāṭṭum1 uṭaittāki varutalumeṉa iruvakaiyum uṭaitteṉṟāṉ; iṉic cūttirat tuṭ poruḷaṉṟiyum orutalaiyāka ataṟku iṉṟiyamaiyātu. poruntuvaṉavellām ataṉōṭu kūṭṭic collutal uraiyeṉappaṭum (e--ṟu),
avai.
iyaṟkaip poruḷai yiṟṟeṉak kiḷattal
	(tol-col-kiḷa : 19)
eṉṟavaḻi eṭuttōttiṉṟi nilam valitāyiṟṟeṉṉum vaḻuvamaiti kōṭalum,
otta cūttiram (653) eṉṟavaḻip,
pāyiram otta cūttirameṉṟu kōṭalum iṉṉōraṉṉa koḷka.
ellāmeṉṟataṉāṟ cūttirapporuḷēyaṉṟi eḻuttuñ collumpaṟṟi ārāyum pakutiyuṭaiyavātal vēṇṭum avvuraiyeṉpatu koḷka.
iṉi, mēṟ kāṇṭikaikku ōtiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷuḷ itaṟ kēṟpaṉavellām atikārattāṟ koḷḷappaṭum. avai 1ētuvum 2naṭaiyum 3eṭuttuk kāṭṭuñ 4cūttiramuñ cuṭṭutalu meṉṟu iṉṉōraṉṉa koḷka. ivaiyellān taḻuvutaṟkup pōlum,
iṉṟi yamaiyā tiyaipavai yellām
eṉṟu eṭuttōtuvāṉāyiṟṟeṉpatu.
āyvurai :
iḵtu uraiyiṉ ilakkaṇam uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) cūttirattiṟ kūṟappaṭum poruḷai viḷakkumaḷavil niṉṟu viṭāmal apporuḷoṭu toṭarpuṟa iṉṟiyamaiyātu avviṭattiṟ koṇarntu uraikkattakkaṉavellām cūttirap poruḷoṭu poruntak kūṭṭiyuraippatu uraiyeṉac ciṟappitturaikkappaṭum e-ṟu.
kuṟṟamillāta cūttirap poruḷait taṉakkuriya ilakkaṇattuḷ oṉṟum maṟaiyātu muṭittuk kūṟuvatum, ataṉaiyē ētuvum eṭuttuk kāṭṭum tantu viḷakkuvatum eṉak kāṇṭikai iruvakaippaṭum eṉa muṉṉuḷḷa irucūttiraṅkaḷāṟ pulappaṭuttiya āciriyar, avviruvakaiyuṭaṉ cūttirap poruḷoṭu toṭarpuṭaiyaṉavāy iṉṟiyamaiyātu koṇarnturaittaṟkuriya ellāp poruḷkaḷaiyum kūṭṭi yuraippatu uraiyeṉac ciṟappitturaikkappaṭum eṉpataṉai iccūttirattāl teḷiyaviḷakkiṉār. eṉavē, cūttirap poruḷai viḷakkumaḷavil niṉṟu viṭāmal apporuḷoṭu toṭarpuṭaiya ellāp poruḷkaḷaiyum koṇarnturaikkum uraiyē akalavuraiyeṉappaṭum eṉpatu pulaṉām. eḻuttum collum paṟṟi ārāyum ārāycciyum ivvuraiyil iṭam peṟum eṉpār, iṉṟiyamaiyātu iyaipavaiyellām oṉṟavuraippatu uraiyeṉappaṭum eṉṟār. ivvurai akalavurai eṉavum viruttiyurai yeṉavum vaḻaṅkappeṟum.	(105)
106.	maṟutalaik kaṭāa māṟṟamu muṭaittāyt
taṉṉū lāṉum muṭintanū lāṉum
aiyamu maruṭkaiyuñ cevvitiṉ nīkkit
teṟṟeṉa oruporuḷ oṟṟumai koḷīit
tuṇivoṭu niṟṟal eṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḷampūraṇam :
ituvumatu.
(i-ḷ) uraiyāvatu, maṟutalaikkaṭāa māṟṟamu muṭaittāka, aiyappaṭṭu niṟṟalu maruṇṭu niṟṟalu nīkki, taṉṉūlāṉātal, apporuṇ muṭivuṟakkūṟiṉa nūlāṉātal teḷiyavoru poruḷai yoṟṟumaippaṭuttu, ituvē poruḷeṉat tuṇital uraiyiṟ kiyalpeṉṟavāṟu.
māṟṟamumuṭaittāki yeṉṟavummaiyāl viṭaiyumuṭait tāki yeṉka.
pērāciriyam :
ituvuṅ kāṇṭikaipōla uraiyum iruvakaitteṉpatu aṟiyumāṟṟāṉ eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti yuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i - ḷ) maṟutalaik kaṭāa māṟṟamum uṭaittāy---maṟutalai māṟṟattiṉai iṭaiceṟittuk kaṭāvutalum ataṟku maṟumāṟṟamākiya viṭai kūṟutalum uṭaittāy: taṉ nūlāṉum --- uraiyeḻutuvāṉāṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟa urai taṉakku mutaṉūlākiya cūttirattāṉum, itaṉāṉē uraiyiṉṟic cūttiramē nūleṉappaṭuvatūu māyiṟṟu; muṭinta nūlāṉum ataṉ mutaṉūlāṉum; muṭintanūleṉpatu iṇainūleṉpārum uḷar: iṇaivaṉa kūṟat tāṉ kūṟāṉāyiṉ atu kuṉṟak kūṟaleṉa maṟukka. aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷum poruṇmaiyuḷavāyiṉ avai,
cūttirat tuṭporu ḷaṉṟiyum
eṉpataṉāṉ aṭaṅkum.
maṟṟu, mutaṉūlāṟ kūṟiya poruḷ cila kuṉṟakkūṟiṉāṉ pōla muṭinta nūlāṟ koḷka. eṉṟateṉṉaiyeṉiṉ aṟṟaṉṟu; ipporuṇmai muṭinta nūliṉum uṇṭeṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭumeṉṟā ṉeṉpatu; itu, mēl;
ētu naṭaiyiṉu meṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉum	(tol-mara: 102)
eṉṟavaḻip peṟutumāyiṉum mutaṉūlallatu eṭuttuk kāṭṭap peṟāareṉṟaṟku īṇṭu varaintu kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu; eṉavē, iṇainūlum avaṟṟu vaḻinūlum eṭuttuk kāṭṭuṅkāl taṉakku mutalvarāyiṉārai nāṭṭi avar karuttē paṟṟip piṟar ceytāreṉiṉallatu piṟarmēl talaimainiṟīi avar karuttuppaṟṟi ivar ceytāreṉak kūṟār. aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉ avar nūṟkē uraiyeḻutuvā ṉallaṉōveṉa maṟukka.
iṉi,
viṉaiyi ṉīṅki viḷaṅkiya vaṟiviṉ muṉaiva
	(tol-mara : 94)
ṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa mutaṉūṟkāyiṉ muṭintanūl eṭuttuk kāṭṭuta leṉṉum ivvilakkaṇamiṉṟeṉpatu koḷka; oru talaiyaṉmaiyeṉṉum uttivakaipaṟṟi yeṉpatu.
aiyamum maruṭkaiyuñ cevvitiṉ nīṅki---aiyavuṇarvum poyyuṇarvuñ cemporuḷiṉāṉ nīkki; teṟṟeṉa oru poruḷ oṟṟumai koḷīi---accemporuḷ karuviyākak kēṭpāṉ uṇarvu maruṭkai nīkki meyyuṇarntu teṟṟeṉavum  iraṭṭuṟutaṉīkki oṟṟumai koḷuttiyum; tuṇivoṭu niṟṟal eṉmaṉār pulavar---kavarpaṭac collātu oruporuḷ tuṇinturaittu māṟutalum atikārattāṉ niṉṟa uraiyilak kaṇam (e-ṟu).
maṟutalaiyum māṟṟamumeṉpatu kaṭā viṭai; aiyamum maruṭkaiyuñ cevvitiṉīkkal orupuṭai yoppumaiyuṭaiya pōliyum, ataṟku oṉṟum iyaipillāta poypporuḷu meṉappaṭum.
piṟitu piṟitēṟṟalu muruputoka varutalum
	(tol-col-vēṟ. ma : 21)
eṉṟavaḻip piṟiteṉṟu orukāṟ colla āṟāmuru peṉavum, piṉṉorukāṟ piṟiteṉa oḻinta urupellān taḻuvu meṉavuñ collutal pōliyeṉappaṭum; eṉṉai? avvāṟāvataṉōṭu ēḻāmurupum piṟvurupēṟkum vaḻakkuḷvaḻi oṉṟaṉaik koṇṭamaiyiṉ aḵtu ōrupuṭai yoppumai yuṭaittāki apporuḷaṉṟeṉavumpaṭātu maṟṟoṉṟiṉaik kōṭal poruḷeṉat tuṇiyavumpaṭātu aiyavuṇarvu ceytamaiyiṉ, atu pōliyāyiṟṟu.
iṉip
piṟitupiṟi tēṟṟalum uruputoka varutalum
eṉṉum iraṇṭummaiyum poruḷilaveṉṟu piṟitu piṟi tēṟṟalum uruputoka varuvataṟkaṇṇēṉṟu poruḷ kūṟutal pōlvaṉa, cūttirattiṉ karuttaṟiyātu poyyai meyyeṉṟu mayaṅkiya maruṭkai yeṉappaṭum. ivviraṇṭaṉaiyum nīkki uṇmai uṇarttutal uraiyeṉappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. niṟṟaleṉpatu niṟka avvurai yeṉṟavāṟu. taṉ nūlāṉum mutaṉūlāṉum aiyamum maruṭkaiyum nīṅkuṅkāl avaṟṟuṭ kiṭanta cem poruḷāṉē nīkkappaṭumeṉpatu iraṇṭu kaṇṇāṉuṅ kūrmaiyāṟ pārttā ṉeṉpatu pōlak koḷka.
āyvurai :
iḵtu, eytiyataṉ mēṟciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) cūttirap poruḷai iṭaimaṟittu viṉavum viṉāvum avviṉāviṟku maṟumoḻiyākiya viṭaiyum uṭaiyatāyt tāṉ urai ceytaṟkuriya nūliṉ cūttirattālum annūliṉ poruṇmai muṟṟa muṭinta nūlākiya mutaṉūlālum aiyavuṇarvaiyum tiripuṇarvaiyum aṟavē nīkkik kavarpaṭu poruḷiṉṟit teḷivāka oruporuḷai oṟṟumaippaṭuttu itaṟku ituvē poruḷ eṉattuṇinturaikka eṉa aṟi vuṟuttuvar pulavar e---ṟu.
maṟutalaik kaṭāa .. iṭaimaṟittut taṭai ceyvatākiya viṉā. māṟṟam --- avviṉāviṟku maṟumoḻiyākiya viṭai. taṉṉūl eṉṟatu, uraiyāciriyaṉ tāṉ uraiyeḻutat tērntu eṭuttuk koṇṭa nūliṉai, muṭinta nūl eṉṟatu, taṉatu nūliṉ poruṇmaiyiṉai muṟṟa muṭittuk kūṟuvatākiya mutal nūliṉai. aiyamāvatu, itaṟkup poruḷ ituvō atuvō eṉa oṉṟiṉun tuṇivupiṟavāta nilaiyiluḷḷa pala talaiyāya uṇarvu. maruṭkai---mayakkam; eṉṟatu oṉṟai maṟṟoṉṟākak koḷḷum tiripuṇarviṉai. cevvitiṉ nīkkalāvatu, itaṟku ituvē poruḷ eṉayāvarum nēritiṉ ēṟṟuk koḷḷumpaṭi aiyattiṉaiyum mayakkattiṉaiyum aṟavē kaḷaital, teṟṟeṉa ---teḷivāka, oruporuḷ oṟṟumai koḷuvutalāvatu, ituvum poruntum atuvum poruntum eṉak kavarpaṭac collāmal, itaṟku ituvē poruḷ eṉapporuḷoṟṟumai kāṭṭit taṇinturaittal.	(106)
107.	collap paṭṭaṉa ellā māṇpum
maṟutalai yāyiṉ maṟṟatu citaivē.
iḷampūraṇam :
(i-ḷ) mēlavaṟṟiṟ kōtalāṉa nūṟkuriyatōr marapu mutaṉūlāyiṟ citaivillai yeṉṟavāṟu,
eṉṉai āvaṉa kūṟiyatu viriyakalātaṉa citaivatu vaḻi nūleṉṟavāṟām.
pērāciriyam :
itu, vaḻinūṟkē āvatōr vēṟupāṭuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i ḷ) mēṟkūṟiya cūttiramuṅ, kāṇṭikaiyum, uraiyu meṉṉum mūṉṟaṟkuñ collappaṭṭa ilakkaṇamellāñ citaiyātu māṭcimaippaṭiṉum, mutaṉūloṭu māṟu koḷḷiṉ avaṟṟāṉ ellāñ citaintateṉavē paṭum annūl e-ṟu.
maṟutalaiyāyiṉu meṉṟa ummai etiratutaḻīiyiṟṟu; mēṟkūṟum pattuvakaik kuṟṟamumē (653) yaṉṟi vaḻinūṟku ituvuṅ kuṟṟamāmeṉṟamaiyiṉ1 īraiṅkuṟṟamumiṉṟi eṉpataṉai nōkka iṟantatu taḻīiyaṟṟumām. itaṉāṉē, piṟ kālattu nūlceyvār nūlilakkaṇam piṟaḻāmaṟceyyiṉum muṟkālattu nūloṭu poruṇ māṟupaṭac ceyyiṉ atu marapaṉṟu vaḻinūṟkeṉa vilakkiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. eṉavē, mutaṉūṟ kāyiṉ ivvārāycciyiṉṟeṉpatu karuttu; eṉṉai? mutalvaṉūṉ māṟupaṭuvataṟku ataṉ muṉṉaiyatōr nūl iṉmaiyiṉeṉpatu. allatūum maṟṟatu eṉṟu orumai kūṟiṉamaiyāṉum itu vaḻinūṟkē vilakkiṟṟeṉpatu koḷka.2 maṟṟu mēl,
mutalum vaḻiyumeṉa nutaliya neṟiyiṉa
	(tol. mara : 39)
eṉavē, mutaṉūliṉ vaḻittu vaḻinūleṉpatūum, maṟutalai yāyiṟ citaiveṉpatūum peṟutumātaliṉ iccūttiram mikaiyām piṟaveṉiṉ, - aṟṟaṉṟu; mutalvaḻiyeṉpaṉa muṉṉum piṉṉum kāṭṭi ṉaṉṟi māṟupaṭāmaik kūṟalvēṇṭu meṉpatūum peṟutumākaliṉ atu kūṟalvēṇṭumeṉpatu peṟāmākaliṉ itu kūṟalvēṇṭumeṉpatu. allākkāl,
		muṉṉōr nūliṉmuṭipuoruṅ kovvāmaip
piṉṉōṉ vēṇṭum vikaṟpaṅ kūṟuvāṉ
cellumeṉpatu.3
āyvurai :
itu vaḻinūṟkē yāvatōr vēṟupāṭu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa ilakkaṇamellām citaiyāmal māṭcimaippaṭiṉum mutaṉūloṭu māṟupaṭavariṉum annūl citaivuṭaiyateṉavē paṭum e-ṟu.
maṟutalaiyātal --- mutalnūlporuḷoṭu māṟupaṭutal citaivu --- kuṟṟam. mēṟkūṟappaṭum kūṟiyatu kūṟal mutalākiya pattuk kuṟṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉ iṅṅaṉam mutaṉūloṭu māṟukoḷḷa variṉum atuvum kuṟṟamākavē koḷḷappaṭum eṉa aṟi vuṟuttuvār. maṟutalaiyāyiṉum maṟṟatu citaivē eṉṟār āyiṉum eṉpuḻi ummai mēṟkūṟappaṭum kūṟiyatu kūṟal mutalākiya kuṟṟappaṭa varutalēyaṉṟi ivvāṟu mutaṉūloṭu māṟupaṭavariṉ kuṟṟamām eṉa etiratu taḻīi vantamaiyiṉ etiratu taḻīiya eccavummaiyākum eṉak koṇṭār pērāciriyar. iṉi, maṟutalaiyāyiṉ eṉṟa pāṭam iḷampūraṇar uraippatippiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.	(107)
108.	citaivila eṉpa mutalvaṉ kaṇṇē
pērāciriyam :
itu mēṟkūṟiya vaḻinūṟkuppōla mutaṉūṟkē āvatōr vēṟupāṭuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ) mēṟ potuvakaiyāṟ kūṟappaṭṭa nūlilakkaṇam aintaṉuḷ īraiṅkuṟṟamumiṉṟiyeṉa muṉṉē ōtiya ilakkaṇam oṉṟu: mutaṉūṟkāyiṉ ammarapiṉa ilakkaṇam vēṇṭuvataṉṟu1 (e-ṟu).
citaivilakkaṇattaic citaiveṉṟu ōtiṉāṉ āku peyarāṉeṉa uṇarka. maṟṟu,
viṉaiyi ṉīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ muṉaivaṟku	(649)
citaivilaveṉpatu aṟivāṉē uṇarvaleṉiṉ, eṭuttōttuk kaḷaintu uyttuṇartal payamiṉṟeṉpatu. allatūum mutaṉūṟku muṉṉaiyatōr nūliṉai ilakkiyamākap peṟiṉaṉṟē mutalvaṉṟāṉ nūlilakkaṇañ ceyvatu.
maṟṟu aṉṉatōr nūliṉ avai ceyyāta nūlilakkaṇam yāṉ ceytēṉeṉavum avaṟṟuc citaiviṉaiyē mutaṉūṟkaṇ illaiyeṉṟatūum, oḻinta nāṉkum mutalvaṉūṟkaṇṇē uḷavākaliṉ avaṟṟai ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṉāṉ eṉavum, iṉik kuṟṟaṅkaḷum piṟṟōṉṟuṅkoleṉṟu añci iṅṅaṉam avaṟṟai varaiyaṟuttu yāṉ pātukākkiṉṟēṉeṉavum, aṅṅaṉam pātukāttu ivvilakkaṇaṅkaḷ ōtiṟṟum avaṉ mutaṉūlē ilakkiyamākap peṟṟaṉavuṅ kūṟiyatu kūṟal (663) pōlvaṉa vēṟōr poruḷ viḷakkumāyiṟ kuṟṟamaṉṟeṉpa taṉaiyum yāṉē kūṟiyataṉṟi mutalvaṉāyiṟ cilvakaiyeḻuttiṉ ceyyuṭṭākavē avaṟṟai vēṟuvēṟu vitittup parantu paṭac ceyyumeṉavum, ivaiyellām aṟivittaṟkuc,
citaivila veṉpa mutalvaṉ kaṇ	(661)
eṉṟāṉeṉpatu.
maṟṟu, mutalvaṉyāppē kūṟumāyiṉ annūṟkut tantiravuttiyum vēṇṭuvateṉṟām piṟaveṉiṉ aṅṅaṉamē, mutaṉūṟkāyiṉ muppattiruvakaiyutti vārātu ciṟupāṉmaiyāṉ varumeṉṟaṟkaṉṟē,
		īraiṅ kuṟṟamu miṉṟi nēritiṉ
muppat tiruvakai yuttiyoṭu puṇarntatu	(653)
eṉa īṇṭu vilakkappaṭṭa kuṟṟattiṉ piṉṉar avvuttiyai vaitta karutteṉpatu āṇṭuṅ kūṟuvāmāyiṟṟeṉpatu.
maṟṟu, mutaṉūliṉāl nūl ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟāṉōveṉiṉ, kūṟiṉāṉē yaṉṟō? tāṉ oruvakaiyāṉ nūlceytu maṟṟu atuvē nūlilakkaṇameṉap piṟkālattārkku aṟiyavait tamaiyiṉeṉpatu.1 maṟṟuc citaivilaveṉpār yārōveṉiṉ nikaḻkālattāciriyarum etirkālattāciriyarumeṉa uṇarka.
āyvurai :
itu mutaṉūṟkuriyatōr marapuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) : mutalvaṉ ceyta nūliṉkaṇṇē kūṟiyatu kūṟal mutaliya kuṟṟamuṭaiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ uḷavākā eṉpar periyōr e-ṟu.
mutalnūl ceytavaṉ viṉaiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ muṉaivaṉ ātalāṉum, avaṉ nūlceytaṟkumuṉ ataṟku mutaṉūlākap piṟitoru nūl iṉmaiyāṉum mutalvaṉ kaṇ citaivu ila eṉpa eṉṟār citaivu - kuṟṟam; citaivuṭaiya ilakkaṇattaic citaivu eṉṟār.
innūṟpāvukku iḷampūraṇarurai kiṭaikkavillai. (108)
109.	mutalvaḻi yāṉum yāppiṉuṭcitaiyum
vallōṉ puṉaiyā vāram pōṉṟē.
iḷampūraṇam :
vaḻinūṟ kuriyatōr marapuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i--ḷ) mutaṉūliṉ vaḻicceyyiṉum annūl yāppiṉuṭ citaiyum vallavaṉ puṉaiyāta vārampōla veṉṟavāṟu.
pērāciriyam :
itu, mutaṉūloṭu maṟutalaippaṭutal vaḻinūṟkuk kuṟṟa meṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) mutalvaḻiyāyiṉum yāppiṉuṭcitaiyum --- atikārattāṉē mutaṉūliṉaik kuṟṟamiṉṟi varuvateṉṟu īṇṭu ilakkaṇaṅkūṟal vēṇṭuvataṉṟeṉṟāṉ iṉi ammutaṉūliṉai māṟupaṭāmal āṇṭōtiya poruṇmai kūṟumāyiṉum, nālvakai yāppiṉuṇ mutaṉiṉṟa mūṉṟumpaṟṟi vaḻi nūṟkuk kuṟṟam piṟakkum; maṟṟu moḻipeyarttaloḻiyak koḷḷumāṟeṉṉai yeṉiṉ, yāppiṉuṭ citaiyu meṉṟu iṭamum iṭattiyal poruḷumāka ōtiṉākaliṉ aḵtoḻikkap paṭum. maṟṟu aḵtoḻikkumāṟeṉṉai yeṉiṉ, --- tamiḻ nāṭṭu vaḻakkiṟku mutaṉūlākiya akattiyattuṇ moḻi peyarttuc ceyyavēṇṭum poruḷilavākalāṉum, piṟa pāṭaikkum potuvāyiṉa poruḷavāyiṉ avaiyun tamiḻ vaḻakku nōkkiyē ilakkaṇañ ceyyappaṭumākalāṉum, avarkkum moḻipeyarttal vēṇṭuvataṉṟākaliṉ atuvē oḻikkappaṭum. ikkaruttiṉāṉaṉṟē,
vaḻiyi ṉeṟiyē nālvakait tākum
	(tol. mara : 96)
eṉa vaḻinūlaiyē virittu nālvakai yāppiṟkum urimai kūṟiyateṉpatu.1 aṅṅaṉam mutaṉūṟku moḻipeyarttaliṉ maiyiṉ yāppoṉṟumpaṟṟic citaivupiṟavātu, oḻintaṉa paṟṟi vaḻinūṟkuc citaivuṇṭāmeṉpatu karuttu.
vallōṉ puṇarā vāram pōṉṟē - vāram puṇarppāṉ vēṟoruvaṉāyavaḻi mutaṟkūṟu puṇarntāṟ puṇarntavāṟṟōṭu poruntac ceyyātavāṟupōla (e-ṟu).
vārameṉpatu kūṟu; eṉṉai? oru pāṭṭiṉaip piṟ kūṟucolluvārai.
vāram pāṭun tōriya makaḷirum
	(cilap 14: 155)
eṉpavākaliṉ,
yāppiṉuṭ citaitaleṉpatu2 mutaṉūlum vaḻakku nūlāyiṉ iḻintōr vaḻakkum vaḻakkaṉṟōveṉak kūṭṭi virittu yāttucceyyiṉum. aḻāṉ puḻāṉ (tol. eḻuttu : 316) eṉpaṉa pōlvaṉa ikkālattilaveṉṟu kaḷaintu tokuttu yāttucceyyiṉun, tokaiviriyum mayaṅkumāṟṟāṉ virintatu tokuttaleṉṉum nūṟpuṇarppiṉait tokaiviriyeṉum yāppeṉak kūṟiyatallātavaḻittokuttu yāttē ceytalum, immūvakai yāppiṉoṭu meyttiṟaṅ kūṟuvaleṉa moḻi peyarttalai mayakkik kūṟutalumellām yāppiṉuṭ citaivēyām. atu paṇṇum pāṇiyum mutalāyiṉa oppiṉum vallōṉ puṇartta iṉṉicaiyataṉ nīrmaippaṭak kāṭṭā; vāram puṇarppāṉ puṟanīratākiya icaipaṭap puṇarttal pōlvatāyiṟṟu.
maṟṟuiḻintōr vaḻakkiṉaip piṟkālattu uyarntōrun takutipaṟṟi vaḻaṅkupavākalāṉ avaiyum amaiyāvōveṉiṉ avai cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkutavātu; kaṟṟuṇarntārum kaṟṟuṇarātārum maṟṟu avai kēṭṭē maṉamakiḻvāraippaṟṟi nikaḻntaṉavāyiṉum, oṟṟumaippaṭṭu oruvakai nillā, peṟṟa kālantōṟum piṟitupiṟitākik kaṭṭaḷaippaṭuttu nūl cey vārkkuṅ kaiyikantu, varaiyaṟai yiṉṟāmākaliṉ aḵtu ilakkaṇameṉappaṭāteṉpatu.1
āyvurai :
itu, vaḻinūṟku āvatōr marapuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) mutaṉūlaiyē aṭiyoṟṟi māṟupaṭāmai aṅku ōtiya poruḷaiyē vaḻinūl kūṟumāyiṉum, icaivallavaṉāṟ puṇarkkappaṭāta vāra icai pōṉṟu, (tokuttal, virittal, tokaiviri, moḻipeyarppu eṉṉum) yāppuvakai kāraṇamāka vaḻinūlil ilakkaṇac citaivu ēṟpaṭutal uṇṭu. (attakaiya citaivu nērātavāṟu vaḻinūl ceyka) e-ṟu.
yāppu eṉṟatu, vaḻi nūliṉ amaippu muṟaiyākiya tokuttal mutaliya nālvakaiyāppiṉai. mutaṉūlāciriyaṉ kūṟiya poruṇmaiyiṉai aṭiyoṟṟi vaḻinūl ceyyuṅkāl tokutturaikkattakkaṉa ivai, viritturaikkat takkaṉa ivai, ivvirutiṟamum amaiyak kūṟattakkaṉa ivaiyeṉṟu ārāyn tuṇarntu, avaṟṟai ammuṟaiyil yāttamaittal kaṟṟut tuṟaipōyiṉārk kallatu ēṉaiyōrkku attuṇai eḷitaṉ ṟākaliṉ mutalvaḻiyāyiṉum yāppiṉuṭ citaiyum eṉṟār. vallōṉ eṉṟatu. icait tuṟaiyil vallavaṉai. vāram eṉpatu, icaippāṭalil muṟkūṟu pāṭuvārkkuriya icaiyuṭaṉ poruntap piṟkūṟupāṭuvōrkkuriyatāka amaikkappeṟum icaiyamaippiṉai, icaittuṟaiyil vallavaṉ allātavaṉāl amaikkappaṭum vāra icai muṟkūṟṟiṉuṭaṉ iṇaitaliṉṟi vaḻuppaṭutal pōṉṟu, nūṟpuṇarp paṟiyātāṉ ceyta vaḻi nūl yāppu vakaiyāl mutaṉūloṭu māṟukoṇṭu vaḻuppaṭum eṉpatām. mutaṉūliṉ coṟporuḷamaippiṉai aṭiyoṟṟi ataṉ vaḻiyil poruḷpiṟaḻāmai moḻi peyarttal vēṇṭum eṉpataṉai moḻi peyarttu atarppaṭa yāttal eṉa muṉṉark kūṟiṉārātaliṉ moḻipeyarppu yāppu oṉṟu nīṅkalāka ēṉaiya tokuttal mutaliya mūṉṟuyāppiṉai iccūttiram cuṭṭiyatākak koḷvar pērāciriyar.	(109)
110.	citaiveṉap paṭupavai vacaiyaṟa nāṭiṟ
kūṟiyatu kūṟal māṟukoḷak kūṟal
kuṉṟak kūṟal mikaipaṭak kūṟal
poruḷila kūṟal mayaṅkak kūṟal
kēṭpōrk kiṉṉā yāppiṟ ṟātal
paḻitta moḻiyāṉ iḻukkak kūṟal
taṉṉāṉ oruporuḷ karutik kūṟal
eṉṉa vakaiyiṉum maṉaṅkōḷ iṉmai
aṉṉa piṟavum avaṟṟuviri yākum.
iḷampūraṇam :
mēlatikārappaṭṭa īraiṅ kuṟṟamum āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ) kūṟiyatu kūṟalāvatu orukāṟ kūṟiyataṉaip piṉṉuṅkūṟal.
māṟukoḷak kūṟalāvatu orukāṟ kūṟiya poruḷōṭu māṟu koḷḷumāṟu piṉkūṟal aḵtāvatu tavamnaṉṟu eṉṟavaṉṟāṉ tavan tīteṉṟu kūṟal.
kuṉṟak kūṟalāvatu tāṉatikaritta poruḷkaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟaik kūṟātoḻital.
mikaipaṭak kūṟalāvatu atikārap poruḷaṉṟip piṟa poruḷuṅ kūṟutal. aḵtāvatu tamiḻilakkaṇañ colluvā ṉeṭuttuk koṇṭāṉ vaṭamoḻiyilakkaṇamum kūṟal.
poruḷila kūṟalāvatu muṉṉum piṉṉum varukiṉṟa poruṇmaik koppiṉṟip payaṉillātaṉa kūṟal.
mayaṅkak kūṟalāvatu kēṭṭārkkup poruḷ viḷaṅku māṟiṉṟik kūṟal.
kēṭpōrkkiṉṉā yāppiṟṟātaleṉpatu poruḷ yākkappaṭṭa cūttirañ cantaviṉpamiṉṟi yiruttal.
paḻittamoḻiyāṉ iḻukkak kūṟalāvatu tāṉoru poruḷai yoruvāypāṭṭāṟ ṟirittup piṟitoru vāy pāṭṭāṟ kūṟutal. akkuṟippu ulakavaḻak kiṉmaiyāṟ piṟarkkup pulappaṭātām; ataṉāl atuvuṅ kuṟṟamāyiṟṟu.
eṉṉavakaiyiṉum maṉaṅkōḷ iṉmaiyāvatu eḻutti ṉāluñ colliṉāṉum poruḷiṉāṉum maṉaṅkoḷḷumāṟu kūṟāmai.
pērāciriyam :
itu, niṟutta muṟaiyāṉē īraiṅ kuṟṟamum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i--ḷ) citaivu eṉappaṭupavai vacaiyaṟa nāṭiṉ kuṟṟa meṉṟaṟkuc ciṟappuṭaiyaṉavaṟṟaik kuṟṟantīra ārāyiṉ ivaṟṟai eṉappaṭupaveṉpateṉ? kuṟṟamātaṟkuc ciṟantilavām piṟaveṉiṉ,---ivvāciriyar eññāṉṟuṅ kuṟṟattiṟku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟārākalāṉ ilakkaṇattōṭu kūṟuntuṇaip payampaṭutalum oruvāṟṟāṟ ciṟappeṉavēpaṭumeṉpatu; allatūum oḻinta ceyyuṭkāyiṉ ivaiyaṉaittum ākāveṉpatu īṇṭē taḻāal vēṇṭumākalāṉuñ ceyyuṭ kāyiṉ avai amaiyāc ciṟappu uṭaimaiyāṉum itu vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷumēyaṉṟi avaṟṟiṉ vēṟupaṭavuñ ceyyappaṭum nūlilakkaṇamākalāṉum eḻuvakai vaḻuppōla amaivaṉavē kūṟātu nūluḷ varappeṟātaṉavum varaintu kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu amaivaṉavaṟ ṟiṉañcārtti oḻintaṉavuṅ kūṟiṉāṉ; allākkāl, avaiyē kuṟṟamāki oḻintaṉa pukutap peṟuvāṉ cellu meṉṟañciyeṉpatu.
vacaiyaṟa nāṭiṉ eṉṟataṉāṉ iṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟṟameṉṟu varaiyappaṭṭaṉavaṟṟaikkoṇṭu pukuntu maṟṟoru poruḷ koḷḷiṉ avai vacaiyaṟṟaṉavāmeṉpatu. avai 1kūṟiyatu kūṟaṉ, 2māṟukoḷakkūṟal, 3mikaipaṭakkūṟal, 4poruḷiṉ moḻitaṉ 5mayaṅkakkūṟal eṉṉum aintumāyiṉa.
1kūṟiyatukūṟal muṉṉorukāṟ colliya poruḷ piṉṉumorukāṟ collutal; atu,
uṭkuvarat tōṉṟu mīrēḻ tuṟaittu	(tol-puṟat: 1)
eṉavum,
vantavīrēḻ vakaiyiṟ ṟākum	(tol-puṟat: 3)
eṉavum irukāṟcolli orukāṟ payaṉkoṇṭavāṟu.
2māṟukoḷakkūṟal
marappeyark kiḷavik kammē cāriyai
	(tol-eḻut-kuṟ: 10)
eṉṟavaḻip pulliṉaiyum marameṉa aṭakkutal. ik karuttu nōkki (yum) (yē) pōlum ; āṇṭu
oṉṟeṉa muṭittal taṉṉiṉa muṭittal
eṉpataṉāṟ pulliṉaiyum marameṉṟu taḻīiyiṉa akkarut teṉpatu
3kuṉṟakkūṟal collappukunta poruḷiṉai ācaṟak kūṟātu oḻiyappōtal ; iḵtōr payaṉpaṭākkuṟṟam.
4mikaipaṭakkūṟal cilvakai yeḻuttiṉ ceyyuṭṭākac ceyyātu cūttirattuṭ cilacoṉ mikaiyākac ceytal pōlvaṉa ; avai,
āyī riyala puṇarnilaic cuṭṭu	(tol-eḻut-puṇa: 5)
eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa. iṅṅaṉam mikaipaṭac ceyyuṅkāṉ muṉṉiṉṟa colliṟku oṉṟum iyaipillaṉa kūṟalākā; eṉṉai?
muppāṟ puḷḷiyu meḻuttō raṉṉa.
	(tol-eḻut-nūṉ 2)
eṉṟavaḻi, avaitām muppāṟ puḷḷiyu meṉap payaṉilai koṇṭu māṟip piṉṉar eḻuttōraṉṉa eṉṟatu āṇṭu iyaipillatōr colleṉṟu ilēcupaṭātu tāṉ collukiṉṟa poruṭkum iyaipupaṭac colliyē mikaip paṭuttal vēṇṭumeṉpatu : eṉṉai?
eḻutteṉappaṭupa	(tol-eḻut-1)
eṉpataṉuṇ mūṉṟumē ciṟappuṭaiyaveṉpatu koṇṭāṉāyiṟ piṉṉar ammūṉṟiṉaiyuñ ciṟappillā muppatiṉōṭum okkumeṉa uvamikkalākāteṉavum, māṭṭēṟṟuc cūttiraṅkaḷā ṉellām oppumai peṟuvatallatu ciṟappiṉmai kāṭṭap paṭātākalāṉum muppaḵtumē ciṟantaṉaveṉṉuṅ karut tiṉāṉāyiṉ mūṉṟuñ ciṟappilaveṉpatu mutaṟcūttirattuṭ peṟappaṭumākaliṉ, piṉṉar ivvilēcu kūṟic ciṟappaḻittal vēṇṭā vākalāṉum palaporuṭkēṟpiṉallatu kōṭala ṉṟi aiyuṟaṟṟōṟuñ cūttirañ ceyyāṉākalāṉum,
eḻuttō raṉṉa	(tol-eḻuttu : 2)
veṉpatu āṇṭu iyaipillatōr colleṉṟataṉai āṇṭup peytu ilēcu koṇṭāṉeṉal ākāteṉpatu.
5poruḷilamoḻital - nūṉutaliya poruḷaṉṟi,
eṉmaṉār pulavar
eṉṟāṟ pōla vaḻinūlvāypāṭṭu māttiraiyē payaṉākac ceyvaṉa.
6mayaṅkakkūṟal - niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉaṉṟi.
atti ṉakara makaramuṉai yillai
	(tol-eḻut-puṇa : 23)
eṉa mayaṅkakkūṟip piṟitoṉṟukōṭal :
7kēṭpōrkkiṉṉā yāppiṟṟātal-cūttiracceyyuḷ kēṭpōrkku iṉṉāticaippac ceytal.
8paḻittamoḻiyāṉ iḻukkaṅkūṟal --- muṭivallāta collāṉum iḻinta collāṉum eṭuttu muṭippaṉavaṟṟai maṟu muṭipupaṟṟi iḻukkaṅ kūṟalāyiṟṟu.
9taṉṉāṉoruporuḷ karutikkūṟal -- muṉṉōrāṟ kūṟavum paṭātu vaḻakkiṉuḷḷatumaṉṟit taṉṉuḷḷē oru poruḷ paṭaittuk kūṟutal; (taṉ) āṉeṉpatu oru colleṉat tāṉeṉpatōr coṟṟāy meyyiṉuḷ uṇara niṉṟatu. taṉṉāṟ ṟāṉoruporuḷpaṭaittukkuṟukac ceytaṉ mūṉṟāvateṉiṉum iḻukkātu. eṉṉai? nutalikkūṟaleṉṉum payaṉilaikkut tāṉeṉṉum peyar veḷippaṭā niṉṟatu veḷippaṭuttukkoḷap peṟutumākaliṉ.
10eṉṉavakaiyiṉu maṉaṅkōḷiṉmai---evvāṟṟāṉum poruḷaṟitaṟku aritākac ceytal ivaināṉkuṅkuṉṟak kūṟaloṭu kūṭṭa aintum eññāṉṟum payaṉpaṭātaṉavāyiṉa.
aṉṉa piṟavum avaṟṟuviri yākum - avaipōlvaṉa piṟavum īraiṅkuṟṟameṉappaṭṭa tokai eṇṇiṟku avvāṟu tokuttaṟkēṟṟa viriyeṇṇām, ivaiyum ivaipōlvaṉa piṟavum (e-ṟu).
virintatu tokutta leṉpataṉāṉ etiratunōkki āṇṭut tokuttāṉātaliṉ īṇṭu avaṟṟait tokai kūṟātu viritteṇṇiṉā ṉeṉpatu.
piṟavum eṉṟataṉāṉ veṟṟeṉat toṭuttaṉ maṟṟoṉṟu virittal ceṉṟu tēyntiṟutal, niṉṟu payaṉiṉmai eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa koḷka. ivai mēl īraiṅ kuṟṟamumeṉa ummaiyāl taḻuviyavaṟṟu viriyāyaṉa. mutaṉūloṭu māṟutalum yāppiṉuṭ citaitalum ivaipōla orō vaḻi vārā annūliṉ muḻuvatūuṅ koḷḷakkiṭantamaiyiṉ īṇṭavai puṟaṉaṭaiyāl taḻuvappaṭṭavāṟeṉpatu. ivai yellāṅ kuṟṟameṉṟu kaḷaiyappaṭuvaṉavāyiṉum īraiṅ kuṟṟamumiṉṟiyeṉa mutaṟcūttirattuḷ ōtippukuntāṉ. allākkāl ivaṟṟuṭcila nūluṭ pukutumāṟum avaṟṟāṟ payaṉkoḷḷumāṟum. iṉmaiyiṉeṉpatu. payaṉkoḷḷap paṭuvaṉa ainteṉpatu mēṟkāṭṭiṉām.
iṉi, appatiṉāṉkuṅ kuṟṟamēyāki varumāṟu:
		taṉmai yuvamai yuruvakan tīvakam
piṉvaru nilaiyē muṉṉa vilakkē
vēṟṟup poruḷvaippu vēṟṟumai yeṉāa
eṉavum,
		uruvaka muvamai vaḻinilai maṭakkē
viricuṭar viḷakkeṉa marīi varuvaṉa
eṉavuñ cila cūttiraṅkaḷai mutaṉiṟīip piṉṉarum avvāy pāṭṭāṉē,
		taṉmai yuvamai yuruvakan tīvakam
piṉvaru nilaiyē muṉṉa vilakkē
vēṟṟup poruḷvaippu vēṟṟumai yeṉṟāṅ
keṇvakaiyiyava ceyyuṭ kaṇiyeṉa
maiyaṟu pulavar vakutturait taṉarē
eṉṟāṟpōlap piṉṉarum avvāṟē cūttirañceytal 1kūṟiyatu kūṟalāyp payaṉpaṭātāyiṟṟu. eṉṉai? ivaṟṟatu vēṟupāṭu tokaic cūttirattut tuṇinteṇṇiyataṉāṉē peṟṟavaḻip piṉṉum avvāṟē maṟṟōr cūttirañ ceytataṉāṟ payantatiṉmaiyiṉeṉpatu.
		aṉṟeṉa vorutalai tuṇinta kuṟṟa
naṉṟaṟi pulavar nāṭṭutaṟkuriya
eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa varaiyātu kuṟṟameṉṟataṉaiyē cāṉṟōrk kāyiṟ kuṟṟamākāteṉṟal 2māṟukoḷak kūṟalām, ceyyuṭ kellām aṇiyilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvāṉ kuṟṟaṅ kūṟum vaḻi varaintu kūṟutaliṉ.
ceyyuṭkuriya poruṭpaṭai yellāvaṟṟuḷḷum nallaṉavun tīyaṉavum ivaiyeṉṟu collappukuntāṉ avaṟṟuṭcila colliyoḻitalum, vaḻakkoṭu meyp poruḷum ārāyva leṉṟu pukuntāṉ avaṟṟuḷ vaḻakkiṟku vēṇṭuva kūṟi meypporuḷārāycci muḻuvatūuñ collātunekiḻntu pōtalum, eṭuttukkoṇṭa nūluṭ kāṭṭum ilakkiyaṅkaḷaic cūttirattāṉ aṭivarai ceyvaṉeṉṟu pukuntu cila maṟuttuc ceytu cilavaṟṟukkuc ceyyātu pōtal pōlvaṉavuṅ 3kuṉṟakkūṟalām. avai muṭinta nūliṟ kaṇṭu koḷka.
		iṭaiyiṭai tiriyā tiyaṉeṟi marapi
ṉuṭaiya karupporu ḷorōvaḻit tuvaṉṟavu
miyaṟkai marapi ṉuripporu ṭōṉṟavum
paṉṉiru kālamum nālvakai yiṭattoṭun
toṉṉeṟi marapiṟ ṟēraṉṟiṉar ceyalē
eṉac cūttirañceytu nāṟpatteṭṭiṉum nāṟpatteṭṭuk kālamun tokka cūttirattāl.
		tuṭaippaṉa tuṭaittuc ceyaṟkai pōla
vaḻiyiṭa ṉoḻittuk kālaṅ kūṟuval
eṉṟu pukuntu akkālattuḷḷuñ ciṟupoḻutu kūṟātu iṭa ilakkaṇamē kūṟi, iṭattiṟkuc curuṅka vēṟu cūttirañceytavāṟu pōlātu ataṟku iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉavellāṅ kuṉṟāmaṟ kūṟātu, ceṉṟupaṭṭa parappiṟṟākac cūttirañceytal pōl vaṉa 4mikaipaṭakkūṟalām. aḵtāvatu iṉṉiḷavēṇileṉpatu,
		taṇṇiḻa laṟalpāṟ ṟaṭaikarait tuṟaiteraṟu
milaṅku mulaikka ṇēyppakkōṅ kaviḻntu
vaṇṭutā tūtun taṇṭaḷirk kāviṟ
parumala rutirntu murukukamaḻ parappiṉ
maṇvayiṟu kuḷirkkun taṇṇaṟuṅ kayattu
niḻaliruḷ naṭuva ṇaḻalavir tāmarait
tāḷkaṟit taruntum vāḷeyiṟ ṟiḷamīṉ
puḷḷuka veṟiya veḷḷeṉṟu piṟaḻum
paḷiṅku nekiḻntaṉṉa tuḷaṅkāt teṇṇīr
taḷirkuṭaintu teviṭṭiya kuyilkuṉintu kuṭippa
maravan tāḻnta karaimarac cāraṟ
ṟēṉāṟu tēṟal vēṉiṟ kaṇṇum
vārār kolleṉa nīrvār kaṇṇoṭu
pulampuṭai makaṭūuk kalaṅkaña reyta
yāṟuṅ kuḷaṉuṅ kāvumāṭi
yōruyir maintaru makaḷiruṅ kaḷippak
kāmaviḻavoṭu kaṉṉiyar nōṟpa
nilavuñ cāntum palavayiṟ payaṉpaṭa
tuṉpak kālan tuṭaittaṉar peṟūu
miṉpak kāla meṉmaṉār pulavar.
eṉṟāṟpōlap paṟṟikkūṟip peruñcūttirañ ceyta vaḻiyum paruvattiṟku varaiyaṟaiyilakkaṇam pōtutaliṉṟi viṭutalāyiṟṟu.
iṉi,
ammūṉ ṟeṉpa maṉṉaic collē
	(tol-col-iṭai : 4)
eṉavum,
appā leṭṭē yummaic collē
	(tol-col-iṭai: 7)
eṉavum,
āyī raintoṭu piṟavumaṉṉa
eṉavum iṉṉōraṉṉa palavum mikaipaṭac ceytār iv vāciriyarākaviṟ piṟkālattu nūlceytārkkum mikaipaṭac ceytal amaiyumeṉpārumuḷar. aṟṟaṉṟu,
yāvayiṉ variṉun tokaiyiṉ ṟiyalā
	(tol col-iṭai : 42)
eṉappaṭṭa eṇṇākaliṉ tokai, kūṟiṉamaiyāṉum vaḻakkātalāṉum, atumutalāka allatu cūttirac ceyyuḷuñ ceyyārākalāṉum, aṅṅaṉan tokaitoṭarākkāl iraṇṭākkappaṭṭu vikāravakaiyāl tokuttāṉāmeṉavum avai pala collākalāl tokukkappaṭuvaṉavalla veṉavuñ colli maṟukka allatūum. maṉṉeṉpatu ōr iṭaiccol, atu tāṉaṭainta coṟporuḷaṉṟit taṉakku vēṟu poruḷiṉ maiyiṟ poruḷ paṟṟi mūṉṟeṉalākātu, palaporuḷoru col eṉappaṭuva taṉṟi, ataṉāṉ ataṉainōkki mūṉ ṟeṉṟāṉallaṉ, aḵtu aṭutta colliṉai maṉṉaic colleṉṟāṉ, avai mūṉṟaṉṟi ettuṇaiyumpalavāyiṉum oṉṟāyiṉum mūṉṟu pakutippaṭumeṉṟu kōṭaṟkeṉpatu. oḻinta iṭaiccollāyiṉum avaṟṟiṟkum iḵtokkum maṉṉaic colleṉpatu vēṟṟumaittokaiyēyaṉṟip paṇputtokaiyākalumuṭaittu. maṉṉaṭutta colliṉaiyum maṉṉeṉa ākupeyarāṟ kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu.
		vaḻakku vaḻippaṭṭa collīṟu tiriyiṉum
paṭaittuk koṇṭa colloṭu civaṇit
tiricol leṉṟē ceppiṉar pulavar
virivaḷaip paṇaittōṇ maṭanal lōyē
eṉṟāṟ pōla iyaipillaṉa kūṟutal 5poruḷilamoḻi talām. ivai oḻinta ceyyuṭkāyiṉ taṭaṅkaṇṇāyeṉṟāṟ pōl varappeṟumeṉṟaṟku ōtteṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, atu ceyyuḷ ceyvār vēṇṭiyavāṟu ceypavākaliṉ īṇṭuc cilvakai yeḻuttiṉ (655) ceyyuṭṭākac ceyyunūṟkē itu vilakkiṉamaiyiṉ ataṉ tiṟattuk kaṭāviṉṟeṉa maṟukka.
6mayaṅkakkūṟaleṉpatu.
mayaṅkā marapi ṉeḻuttumuṟai kāṭṭuval	(pāyiram)
eṉṟu pukuntāṟpōla, iṉṉatu colluvateṉṟu pukuntāṉ meynnūlum vaḻakkunūlum uṭaṉārāytalum, āciriyamun tamiḻum pōlvaṉa uṭaṉārāytalum pōlvaṉa.
7kēṭpōrkkiṉṉāyāppāvatu,
katana pama
cañayava
uyirmuṉ piṉvalliṉam
larayantā meṉa
eṉṟāṟpōlac cūttiraccurukkamum, moḻikku mutalā meḻuttum īṟāmeḻuttum potuvakaiyāṉ aṭaṅka ōtutaṟ payaṉōkki iṅṅaṉam iṉṉāvōcaittākac cūttirañ ceytal pōlvaṉa.
8paḻittamoḻiyā ṉiḻukkaṅkūṟal,
		viḷāme ṉiṟuti paḻamoṭu puṇariṟ
ṟaḷāviyaṟ ṟaṉṟi yiyaṟkai yākum
eṉṟāṟpōla viḷāmeṉpatōr vaḻuccollāṟ cūttirañ ceytal. oṉṟaṟkoṉṟeṉpataṉai,
oṉṟiṉuk koṉṟu
eṉac cūttirañ ceytalum atu.
9taṉṉāṉoruporuḷ karutik kūṟaleṉpatu,
malaipaṭukaṭāttiṉai āṉantak kuṟṟameṉap piṟ kālattāṉoruvaṉ oru cūttiraṅ kāṭṭutalum, patamuṭip peṉpatōr ilakkaṇam paṭaittuk kōṭalum (naṉṉūl) pōlvaṉa.
10eṉṉa vakaiyiṉu maṉaṅkōḷiṉmai varumāṟu :
irutiṇaip piṟanta vaimpāṟ kiḷavikkum
	(tol-col-peyar: 7)
eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṟkum,
eppeyar muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi
	(tol-eḻut-puṇar: 26)
eṉpataṟkum vēṟuporuḷ uraippāruraikkumāṟṟāṉ aṟiyappaṭum.
11iṉi maṟṟoṉṟu virittaleṉpatu.
āṟuṟuppum pāviṉamuṅ kūṟuvaleṉṟu pukuntu poruḷārāyacci palavuṅ kūṟutal. ituvum iṉṉōraṉṉavum mikaipaṭak kūṟalāy aṭaṅkumeṉiṉum amaiyum.
12niṉṟupayaṉiṉmai yeṉpatu.
piṟkālattut tōṉṟiya vaḻakkēpaṟṟi avaṟṟiṟkuṅ kuṟṟantīra ilakkaṇaṅ kāṭṭiyavaḻi, atu cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkup payaṉpaṭātu tammōraṉṉa ceyyuṭkup payaṉpaṭṭoḻiyac ceytal. avai vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka. pāṭṭiyaṉ marapeṉak kāṭṭuvaṉavumavai.
13iṉi veṟṟeṉat toṭutta leṉpatu.
kēṭpōrk kiṉṉā yāppiṉpāṟ paṭṭu aṭaṅkum.
14ceṉṟutēyntiṟuta leṉpatu.
collappukuntatoru poruḷ ellāviṭattuñ collap paṭuvatākavum īṟṟukkaṇ māyntu māṟuvatu, atuvuṅ kuṉṟak kūṟalāy aṭaṅkum oḻintaṉavum aṉṉa.
maṟṟuk kūṟiyatukūṟaṉ muṉvaittateṉṉaiyeṉiṉ --atu nūluṭpōlac ceyyuṭkaṇṇum payaṉpaṭavariṉ amaiyu meṉṟaṟ keṉpatu. avai:
vaikalum vaikal varakkaṇṭu maḵtuṇarār	(nālaṭi-4: 9)
eṉavum,
vañciyāy vañciyār kō	(yā-vi-pa; 230)
eṉavuñ colvakaiyāṉ iraṭṭittavāṟu, iṉiyamaivaṉa vaṟṟiṭaiyē kuṉṟakkūṟal vaittāṉ avaṟṟuttuṇaic ciṟappilaveṉṟu aiyuṟāmai yeṉpatu.
ivvāciriyar yāṇṭum ilakkaṇamē kūṟi ivvilak kaṇattiṟ piṟaḻavarutalaik kuṟṟameṉṟu koḷḷa laippiṉaṉṟi illāta kuṟṟaṅkaṭku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟātār1 itu kūṟiṉār.
etirmaṟut tuṇariṉ ṟiṟattavu mavai	(664)
eṉa varukiṉṟa cūttirattāṉ ivaiyum ilakkaṇattiṟku upakārappaṭutali ṉeṉpatu2.
āyvurai :
iḵtu īraiṅkuṟṟam (pattuk kuṟṟaṅkaḷ) ivaiyeṉak kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) nūṟkuc citaiveṉak kūṟappaṭum kuṟṟaṅkaḷaip paḻitīra ārāyiṉ, kūṟiyatukūṟal, māṟukoḷakkūṟal, kuṉṟak kūṟal, mikaipaṭakkūṟal, poruḷilamoḻital, mayaṅkakkūṟal, kēṭpōrkku iṉṉā yāppiṟṟātal, paḻitta moḻiyāṉ iḻukkam kūṟal, taṉṉāṉ oruporuḷ karutikkūṟal, eṉṉa vakaiyiṉum maṉaṅkōḷiṉmai eṉṉum ippattum avai pōlvaṉa piṟavum īraiṅ kuṟṟam eṉa muṉṉart tokut turaikkappaṭṭa akkuṟṟaṅkaḷiṉ viriyākum e-ṟu.	(110)
111.	etirmaṟut tuṇariṉat tiṟattavum avaiyē.
iḷampūraṇam :
ituvumatu. nūṟkuṟṟam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ) etirmaṟuttu uṇarvarāyiṉ, attiṟattavum kuṟṟamā meṉṟavāṟu.
pāvañ ceytāṉ niraiyam pukumeṉak karutik kūṟuvāṉ tavañceyvāṉ cuvarkkam pukumeṉṟal. ivvāṟu kūṟic cuvarkkam peṟumeṉṉum poruṭ ... niraiyampukumeṉṟa poruḷ tōṉṟāmaiyiṟ kuṟṟamāyiṟṟu.
pērāciriyam :
itu, mēṟ kuṟṟam pattuṅ kūṟi iṉik kuṇamum pattuḷa veṉkiṉṟatu. mēlellām ivvāciriyaṉ ilakkaṇa vaḻakkiṉaiyē vitantōti ataṉiṟpiṟaḻntatu kuṟṟameṉṟu koḷḷa vaittāṉ; ivvōttiṉuḷ īraiṅkuṟṟamu meṉpaṉa cila kuṟṟaṅ kūṟiṉāṉ1 ; itu māṟukoḷak kūṟalāṅ kollō veṉiṉ aṟṟaṉṟu; ivaiyum ilakkaṇamē kūṟiṉaṉeṉpāṉ ivaṟṟai yetirmaṟuttuk koḷḷa veṉṟāṉeṉpatu. itaṉatu payaṉ; uḷḷatu collutalēyaṉṟi illatu collutalum nūlilakkaṇameṉṟaṟivitta lāyiṟṟu; avai
peyarnilaik kiḷavi kālan tōṉṟā
	(tol-col-vēṟ : 9)
eṉavum,
viṉaiyeṉap paṭuvatu vēṟṟumai koḷḷātu
	(tol-col-viṉai: 1)
eṉavum varum. eṉavē, kūṟiyatu kūṟāmaiyum māṟu koḷak kūṟāmaiyuṅ kuṉṟakkūṟāmaiyum pōlvaṉavum pattu uḷa nūlilakkaṇameṉṟāṉām. mēl,
īraiṅ kuṟṟamumiṉṟi
eṉṟatallatu avaṟṟai nāṭṭikkoṇṭu etirmaṟuttuk kōṭal peṟāmaiyiṉ itu kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu.
āyvurai :
itu nūlukkuriya kuṇaṅkaḷāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i--ḷ) mēṟkuṟitta pattuk kuṟṟaṅkaḷaiyum etir maṟuttu uṇarntu koḷḷutaṟkuriya kuṇaṅkaḷ pattum mēṟ kuṟitta nūlukkuriya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷām e-ṟu.
nūlukku amaiya vēṇṭiyaṉavākavuḷḷaṉavaṟṟai eṭuttuk kūṟutalōṭu, nūluṭ pukutaṟku uriyaṉavaṟṟai eṭuttuk kūṟutalum nūṟkuriyatōr ilakkaṇamē eṉpataṉai aṟivuṟut tuvār nūlil iṭam peṟāta kuṟṟaṅkaḷaiyum eṭuttuk kūṟiṉār. āciriyar. eṉavē, muṟkūṟiya pattuk kuṟṟaṅkaḷai etir maṟuttuk koḷḷappaṭum pattuk kuṇaṅkaḷum nūlukkuriya ilakkaṇamām eṉpār etir maṟuttuṇariṉ tiṟattavum avaiyē eṉṟār. mēṟkuṟitta kuṟṟaṅkaḷaiyetir maṟuttuk koḷḷutalāvatu, kūṟiyatu kūṟāmai, māṟukoḷak kūṟāmai kuṉṟak kūṟāmai, mikaipaṭak kūṟāmai, poruḷila moḻi yāmai, mayaṅkakkūṟāmai, kēṭpōrkku iṉṉā yāppiṉai yuṭaiyatākāmai, paḻitta moḻiyāṉ iḻukkam kūṟāmai, taṉṉāṉ oru poruḷ karutik kūṟāmai, eṉṉavakaiyiṉum maṉaṅ kōḷiṉmaiyillāmai eṉa ivvāṟu koḷḷutal.
		curuṅkac collal viḷaṅka vaittal
naviṉṟōrk kiṉimai naṉmoḻi puṇarttal
ōcai yuṭaimai āḻamuṭait tātal
muṟaiyil vaippē ulakamalaiyāmai
viḻumiyatu payattal viḷaṅkutāraṇatta
tākutal nūliṟ kaḻakeṉum pattē
eṉa varum naṉṉūl-pāyiram-mayilai nātar uraimēṟ kōṭ cūttiram nūlukkuriya kuṇaṅkaḷākiya pattaḻakiṉaiyum virittuk kūṟuvatākum.	(111)
112.	otta kāṭci uttivakai virippiṉ
nutaliya taṟital atikāra muṟaiyē
tokuttuk kūṟal vakuttumeyn niṟuttal
moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟavaittal
moḻiyā tataṉai muṭṭiṉṟi muṭittal
vārā tataṉāṉ vantatu muṭittal
vantatu koṇṭu vārātatu muṭittal
muntu moḻintataṉ talaitaṭu māṟṟē
oppak kūṟal orutalai moḻiyē
taṉkōṭ kūṟal uṭampoṭu puṇarttal
piṟaṉuṭam paṭṭatu tāṉuṭam paṭutal
iṟantatu kāttal etiratu pōṟṟal
moḻivām eṉṟal kūṟiṟ ṟeṉṟal
tāṉkuṟi yiṭutal orutalai yaṉmai
muṭintatu kāṭṭal āṇai kūṟal
palporuṭ kēṟpiṉnallatu kōṭal
tokutta moḻiyāṉ vakuttaṉar kōṭal
maṟutalai citaittut taṉtuṇi puraittal
piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal aṟiyā tuṭampaṭal
poruḷiṭai yiṭutal etirporuḷa uṇarttal
colliṉ eccam colliyāṅ kuṇarttal
tantupuṇarn turaittal ñāpakam kūṟal
uyttukkoṇ ṭuṇarttaloṭu meyppaṭa nāṭic
colliya alla piṟavavaṇvariṉum
colliya vakaiyāṟ curuṅka nāṭi
maṉatti ṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintu koṇṭu
iṉattiṟ cērtti uṇarttal vēṇṭum
nuṉittaku pulavar kūṟiya nūlē.
iḷampūraṇam :
tantiravutti yāmā ṟuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poḻippu : nutaliya taṟital mutalākac collap paṭṭaṉavum attaṉmaiya piṟavun tantira uttiyām eṉṟavāṟu.
tantirameṉiṉum nūleṉiṉum okkum. uttiyeṉpatu vaṭa moḻic citaivu. atu cūttirattiṉpāṟ kiṭappatōr poruḷ vēṟupāṭu kāṭṭuvatu.
(i-ḷ.) otta kāṭci uttivakai virippiṉeṉpatu nūṟkup poruntiya kāṭciyiṉā ṉuraikkum uttivakaiyai virikkuṅkālat teṉṟavāṟu.
nutaliyataṟitalāvatu --- cūttirattiṟ coṟṟa poruḷuṇart talaṉṟi, itaṉkarut tituveṉa uṇarttal.
aḵtāvatu eḻutteṉappaṭupa (nūṉmarapu : 1) eṉṉuñ cūttirattuḷ eḻuttu iṉaitteṉa varaiyaṟuttuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu eṉṟal.
atikāra muṟaiyāvatu muṉṉam palaporuḷai yatikaritta vaḻip piṉṉum ammuṟaiyiṉāṉē virittuṇarttutal.
aḵtāvatu, uyartiṇai yaḵṟiṇaiyeṉa atikarittu āṭūu vaṟicol (kiḷaviyākkam - 2) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ niṟuttamuṟai piṟaḻāmal uyartiṇaikūṟal. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē oru cūttirattilē karutiṉa poruḷai vaittu varukiṉṟa cūttirattuḷ ōtātu ataṉ kāriyamāyiṉa kūṟiyavaḻi ataṉaic cūttirantōṟuṅ koṇarnturaittal. aḵtāvatu akara viṟutip peyarnilai muṉṉar (uyirmayaṅkiyal-1) eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṟ kacatapat tōṉṟi ṉeṉavōti viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyu, (uyirmaṅkiyal - 2) meṉṉuñ cūttirattuḷ ōtiṟṟila rāyiṉum. atikayāramuṟaimaiyiṉāṉ valleḻuttuvaruvaḻi yeṉa vuraittal
tokuttuk kūṟalāvatu---vakaipeṟak kūṟal vēṇṭu māyiṉum ataṉait tokuttuk kūṟal.
eḻutteṉap paṭupa akaramuta ṉakara viṟuvāymuppaḵ teṉpa (nūṉ marapu, 1) eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa iṉṉum pala cūttirattāṟ kūṟiya poruḷai ittuṇaiyuṅ kūṟappaṭṭatitu veṉak kūṟalumām. tūkkiyal vakaiyē yāṅkeṉa moḻipa (ceyyuḷiyal-83) eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka.
vakuttu meynniṟut talāvatu---tokaipaṭak kūṟiya poruḷai vakaipaṭak kūṟal.
atu a, i, u, e, o (nūṉmarapu, 3) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṟ koḷka. iṉṉumataṉāṉē tokaipaṭac cūttirañceyta vaḻi avaṟṟuḷ orōvoṉṟu potuvilakkaṇattāṉ muṭiyātavaḻip perumpāṉmai ciṟupāṉmaikoṇṭu vakuttup poruḷurait talumām. iṉṉumataṉāṉē tokaipaṭak kūṟiyataṉai vakuttup poruḷuraittalumām.
moḻinta poruḷōṭoṉṟavaitta lāvatu --- cūttirattuṭporuḷ palapaṭat tōṉṟumāyiṉ muṟpaṭṭa cūttirat tiṟkokkum poruḷuraittal.
aṉṟiyum muṟpaṭṭa cūttirattiṉāṉ oruporuḷōtiya vaḻip piṟpaṭṭa cūttiramum poruḷōṭoṉṟavaittalumām.
moḻiyā tataṉai muṭṭiṉṟi muṭittalāvatu---eṭut tōtāta poruḷai muṭṭuppaṭāmal uraiyiṉāṉ muṭittal.
itaṉai uraiyiṟkōṭal eṉpa. ikkaruttiṉāṉē,
cūttirat tuṭporu ḷaṉṟiyum yāppuṟavu
iṉṟi yamaiyā tiyaipavai yellām
oṉṟa vuraippa turaiyeṉap paṭumē.	(marapiyal-105)
eṉa ōtuvārāyiṟṟeṉka
vārā tataṉāṉ vantatu muṭitta lāvatu---oruṅkeṇ ṇappaṭṭa poruḷoṉṟaṉaip pakuttuk kūṟiyavaḻi āṇṭu vārātataṟkōtiya vilakkaṇattai itaṉkaṇṇum varuvit tuṇarttutal.
vantatukoṇṭu vārātatu muṭittalāvatu---oruṅ keṇṇappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷoṉṟaip pakuttu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiya vaḻi vārātataṉkaṇṇum ivvilakkaṇattaik kūṭṭimuṭittal.
muntu moḻintataṉ talaitaṭumāṟ ṟāvatu --- muṟpaṭa atikaritta poruḷai yavvakaiyiṉāṟ kūṟātu muṟaipiṟaḻak kūṟutal.
ivvāṟu kūṟuṅkāl orupayaṉōkkik kūṟal vēṇṭum atu puḷḷimayaṅkiyaluṭ kaṇṭukoḷka.
oppakkūṟaleṉpatu---oru poruḷeṭuttu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiya vaḻi atupōlvaṉavaṟṟaiyu milakkaṇattāṉ muṭittal.
orutalaimoḻiyāvatu---ēkākkara meṉṉum vaṭa moḻip poruṇmai. aḵtāvatu, cūttirattiṟkup poruḷ kavarttut tōṉṟiṉ ataṉuḷoṉṟaṉait tuṇintu kūṟal.
taṉkōṭ kūṟalāvatu --- piṟanūlāciriyar kūṟiyavāṟu kūṟātu taṉkōṭpāṭṭāl kūṟutal.
atu vēṟṟumai eṭṭeṉṟal.
uṭampoṭu puṇarttalāvatu --- ilakkaṇa vakaiyāṉ ōtutalaṉṟi yāciriyaṉukkiṉṟic cūttirattiṉkaṇṇē yoru collai vaippaṉāyiṉ avvaippiṉai ilakkaṇamākak kōṭal.
oṟṟīṟṟuc collai yukaraṅkoṭuttuk kūṟukaveṉa vilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṟṟilarāyiṉum ārum aruvum īroṭu civaṇum viḷi marapu (21) eṉa ōtutaliṉ, ār eṉpatu ārum eṉa ukaram peṟṟatu. itaṉaip piṟāṇṭuṅ kōṭal.
piṟaṉuṭam paṭṭatu tāṉuṭampaṭutalāvatu --- piṟa nūlāciri yaṉ  uṭampaṭṭa poruṭkut tāṉuṭampaṭutal.
aḵtāvatu iraṇṭām vēṟṟumai ceyappaṭuporuṭ kaṇ varumeṉap pāṇiṉiyār ōtiṉār ; aḵtu ivarkkum uṭampāṭu.
iṟantatu kāttalāvatu --- mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa cūttirattāṟ kūṟappaṭāta poruḷaip piṉvarukiṉṟa cūttirattā ṉamaittal.
etiratu pōṟṟalāvatu --- muṉ kūṟappaṭṭa cūttirat tāṉē varukiṉṟa cūttirattiṟ poruḷiṉaiyum pātukākkumāṟu vaittal.
moḻivāmeṉṟalāvatu --- cila poruḷaik kūṟi avaṟṟuḷoṉṟaṉai yiṉṉaviṭattuk kūṟuvāmeṉa vuraittal.
puṇariya ṉilaiyiṭaik kuṟukalum	(moḻimarapu. 2)
eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka.
kūṟiṟṟeṉṟalāvatu --- pala poruḷā yatikarittavaṟṟuṭ cila poruḷai mēṟcollappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟal.
māttirai vakaiyum eḻuttiyal vakaiyu mēṟkiḷan taṉṉa (ceyyuḷiyal.2) eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka.
tāṉkuṟiyiṭutalāvatu --- ulakiṉkaṇ vaḻakkiṉṟi yoru poruṭku āciriyaṉṟāṉ kuṟiyiṭal.
aḵtu uyartiṇai yaḵṟiṇaiyeṉpaṉa.
orutalaiyaṉmai muṭintatu kāṭṭalāvatu oru poruḷai yōtiya vaḻic colluvataṟkē yurittaṉṟip piṟa poruṭkum potuvāka muṭittamai kāṭṭal.
āṇai kūṟalāvatu --- oru poruḷaik kūṟumvaḻi ētuviṉāṟ kūṟalaṉṟit taṉ ṉāṇaiyāṟ kūṟal.
vēṟṟumai yēḻeṉap pāṇiṉiyār kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ avar viḷiyai mutalvēṟṟumaiyi laṭakkiṉār. ataṟkut tiripukūṟātu ataṉai eṭṭām vēṟṟumaiyeṉṟal āṇṭuk kaṭāvappaṭā.
palporuṭ kēṟpiṉ nallatu kōṭalāvatu---oru cūttiram palaporuṭ kēṟkumāyiṉ avaṟṟuḷ nallataṉaip poruḷākak kōṭal.
tokutta moḻiyāṉ vakuttaṉar kōṭalāvatu---tokuttuk kūṟiya col taṉṉāṉē piṟitumoru poruḷ vakuttukkāṭṭal.
atu kuṟṟiyalukā muṟaippeyar maruṅkiṉ	(viḷimarapu, 9)
eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟukaramuṅ kōṭal.
colliṉ muṭipiṉ apporuṇ muṭittaleṉpatumatu.
maṟutalai citaittut taṉṟuṇi puraittalāvatu---piṟa nūlāciriyaṉ kūṟiṉa poruṇmaiyaik keṭuttut taṉṟuṇivu kūṟutal.
aḵtāvatu neṭṭeḻuttēḻ aḷapeṭaiyeṉpaṉa kuṟ ṟeḻuttiṉ vikārameṉpārai maṟuttu vēṟōreḻuttāka vōtutal.
piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟalāvatu---piṟa nūlāciriyaṉ koṇṭa kōṭpāṭṭaik kūṟutal.
atu vēṟṟumai tāmē yēḻeṉa moḻipa
	(vēṟṟumai yiyal. 1) eṉṟal.
aṟiyā tuṭampaṭalāvatu---tāṉaṟiyāta poruḷaip piṟar kūṟiyavāṟṟā ṉuṭampaṭutal.
atu ēḻānarakam ittaṉmait teṉa voruvaṉ kūṟiyavaḻi atu pulaṉākātātaliṉ avaṉ coṉṉataṟ kuṭampaṭutal; itu vaḻinūlāciriyark kurittu.
poruḷiṭai yiṭutalāvatu---oruporuḷai yōtiya vaḻiyataṟkiṉamākiya poruḷaic cērakkūṟātu iṭaiyīṭupaṭak kūṟutal.
atu peṇmai cuṭṭiya (peyariyal. 24) veṉṉuñ cūttiramōti ataṉ pakutiyākiya āṇmai tirinta peyar nilaik kiḷavi yeṉpataṉai iṭaiyiṭṭu vaittal pōlvaṉa.
etirporu ḷuṇarttalāvatu --- iṉik kūṟavēṇṭuvatitu veṉavuṇarttal.
colliṉ eccam colliyāṅ kuṇarttaleṉpatu --- pirinilai mutalākac collappaṭṭa eccaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭu āṅkuc colliyavāṟṟāṟ poruḷkōṭal.
tantupuṇarnturaittalāvatu --- muṉṉāyiṉum piṉṉā yiṉum niṉṟa cūttirattiṟ collai iṭainiṉṟa cūttirattiṉuṅ koṇarntu puṇarnturaittal.
ñāpakaṅ kūṟalāvatu --- iraṭṭuṟamoḻintu iraṇṭu coṟkum poruḷkōṭal.
uyttukkoṇṭuṇartalāvatu --- oru cūttirattāṉ ōrilakkaṇam ōtiyavaḻi ataṟkup poruntāmai yuḷatākat tōṉṟiṉ ataṟkup poruntumāṟu vicārittuṇartal
paṉiyeṉṉuñ collukku attum iṉṉuñ cāriyaiyā meṉṟārāyiṉum (uyirmayaṅkiyal 39) avaṟṟuḷ eṟpa toṉṟātaliṉ iṉṉīṟṟāyavāṟu varuvaṉa vuyttuṇartalām.
ivai muppattiraṇṭun tantiravuttiyāvaṉa.

pōraciriyam :
itu, muṟaiyāṉē iṟutikkaṇiṉṟa muppattiruvakai yuttiyuṅ kūṟi maṟṟum innūluḷ atikāram mūṉṟaṟkum vēṇṭum puṟaṉaṭaiyuṅ kūṟutaṉutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ) : ottakāṭci yuttivakai virippiṉ---muṟkūṟiya kuṟṟaṅkaḷōṭu oppattōṉṟun tōṟṟattiṉai uṭaiyavākiya uttik kūṟṟiṉai virittuc colliṉ.
pattuvakaik kuṟṟattōṭum ottuvarumeṉavē ivaiyum nūṟkaṇaṉṟi oḻinta ceyyuṭku varuṅkāl vilakkappaṭutalum. muṟkūṟiya kuṟṟampōla ivaiyum vēṟu cila poruḷ paṭaittalumuṭaiyavāyiṉa. kāṭciyutti yeṉṟu ivaṟṟaik kūṟiyavataṉāṉ nūluṭ kāṇappaṭumeṉṟa aintu kuṟṟattōṭum ottal koḷḷappaṭum; avvannūṟkup payampaṭavarum pakutiyāṉeṉpatu.
utti yeṉpatu, nūlceyyuṅkāl iyalpuvakaiyākiya vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum pōlac cevvaṉañ collutal oṇmai  yuṭaittaṉṟām piṟaveṉiṉ aṟṟaṉṟu; avvāṟu ceytakkāl,
nuṇmaiyoṭu puṇarnta oṇmaittākal vēṇṭum			(655) 
eṉpatu muṉṉarccolliṉāṉ. īṇṭuc cevvaṉañ collāta tantiravutti vakaiyum avvāṟē oṇmaiyuṭaiya vāmeṉpatu karuttu; eṉṟārkkuc cevvaṉañ ceytalai uttiyeṉṉāṉō veṉiṉ-atu collāmai  muṭintatākali ṉaṉṟē utti yeṉṉātu ivaṟṟai uttivakaiyeṉpāṉāyiṟ ṟeṉpatu; aḵtēl, ivaṟṟai muṉ tokuttāṉpōla virippiṉeṉṟa teṉṉaiyeṉiṉ muṉṉar etiratunōkki (marapiyal :98) muppattiraṇṭeṉat tokaikūṟippiṉṉar,
		maṉatti ṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇṭu
iṉattiṟcērtti yuṇarttal vēṇṭum
eṉkiṉṟāṉākalāṉ, aṅṅaṉam iṉampaṟṟi avaṟṟōṭu aṭaṅkuvaṉavellām avaṟṟu viriyātumeṉṉuṅ karuttiṉāṟ kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu.
		avaṉiva ṉuvaṉeṉa varūum peyaru
mavaḷiva ḷuvaḷeṉa varūum peyaru
mavariva ruvareṉa varūum peyarum
yāṉyā nāmeṉa varūum peyarum
yāvaṉ yāvaḷ yāvareṉṉu
māvayiṉ mūṉṟō ṭappati ṉaintum
pālaṟi vanta vuyartiṇaip peyarē
	(tol col-peyar :8)
eṉpataṉuḷ,
āvayiṉ mūṉṟō ṭappati ṉaintum
eṉat tokuttuk kūṟiyavaḻi atu mikaipaṭak kūṟalākātu; eṉṉai? ummaiyeṇṇākaliṉ tokaiyiṉṟiyuñ cilvakai yeḻuttiṟ ceyyuṭṭāka vaḻakkiyalāṉē curuṅkacceyvatōr āṟuḷatāyiṉum appatiṉaintumeṉat tokuttuk kūṟal uttivakaiyāṉ amaiyumākaliṉeṉpatu karuttu. iṅṅaṉan tokaikūṟutal veḷḷitaṉṟi oḷḷitākal; avai, patiṉaintu peyarumē orunikaraṉaveṉpatu aṟiyalākumeṉpatu.
iṉi,
meypeṟu marapiṟ ṟoṭaivakai tāmē
	(tol-cey :101)
eṉṉuñ cūttirampōlvaṉa piṟitoruporuḷ payakku meṉṟalum accūttirattuṭ kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. ivvāṟṟāṉ ivvuttivakai ellā nūṟkum iṉṟiyamaiyāvāyiṉa.
maṟṟu, vakaiyeṉappaṭātu cevvaṉañceyyum utti yāvaṉa yāvaiyeṉṉiṉ.
iyaṟkaip poruḷai yiṟṟeṉak kiḷattal
	(tol-col-kiḷa :19)
eṉṟāṟpōlac colliyoḻivaṉa, piṟavum aṉṉa.
virippi ṉeṉṟa viṉaiyeccañ cērtti yuṇarttal vēṇṭu meṉṟa muṟṟuviṉaikoṇṭu muṭintatu.  meyppaṭa nāṭi yeṉpaṉa pōlvaṉavaṟṟukkum ituvē muṭipu; ataṟku nūleṉṉum peyarkoṭuttu muṭikka, nūlaiyeṉṟu aikāram virippiṉ, vēṇṭumeṉpatu taṉpālāṉum piṟaṉpālāṉum peṟṟatōr peyarkoṇṭu muṭiyum.
(1)	nutaliyataṟital---cūttirattuḷ ōtiya poruḷāṟ collappaṭum payaṉ illatupōlac colliyataṉāṉē ataṟkēṟṟavakaiyāṟ karutiyuṇarappaṭuporuḷ iṉṉateṉṟu koḷḷa vaittal: atu,
vēṟuviṉaip potucco loruviṉai kiḷavār
	(tol-col-kiḷavi :46)
eṉavum,
		ciṟappi ṉākiya peyarnilaik kiḷavikku
miyaṟpeyark kiḷavi muṟpaṭak kiḷavār
	(tol-col-kiḷavi :41)
eṉavum ivai vaḻuvaṟka vaḻuvamaikka eṉṉum irupakutiyuḷ vaḻuvamaittaṟkeḻuntaṉaveṉpatu karutiṉāṉ  āciriyaṉeṉpatu aṟiyavantaṉa. itaṉai mutaṟkaṇ vaittuppōy iṟutik kaṇṇē uyttukkoṇṭuṇartalai vaittāṉ ituvum atupōl uyttukkoṇṭuṇartal oruvakaiyāṉuṭait tāyiṉum ataṉāṟ peṟṟa payaṉ piṟitumoṉṟu uḷatātal vēṟṟumai yuṭaimaiyiṉeṉpatu; atu muṉṉarccollutum.
iṉi, mūvakait tamiḻvaḻakkamum nutaliyatum avaṟṟuḷ iyaṟṟamiḻē nutaliyatum, atikāramnutaliyatum, atikārattuḷ ottu nutaliyatum, ōttiṉuṭ cūttiram nutaliyatumeṉavum ivai nutaliyataṟitaṟ pakutiyāy aṭaṅkumeṉpāṉ 
		maṉatti ṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇṭu
iṉattiṟ cērtti yuṇarttal vēṇṭum
eṉkiṉṟāṉeṉpatu, mēlvarukiṉṟavaṟṟukkumokkum. aḵtēl itaṉai āṇṭuvaittu maṟṟataṉai īṇṭu vaippiṉum iḵtokkum piṟaveṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu; nūliṉvēṟākiya pāyirattuḷḷāyiṉum innutaliyataṟital varutalum utti yeṉṟaṟku itaṉai muṉvaittāṉeṉpatu.
2)	atikā ramuṟaimai --- eṉpatu muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirap poruṇmai piṉvaruñ cūttirattiṟkum peṟaṟpālaṉa peṟa vaittal;avai,
iyaṟkaip poruḷai yiṟṟeṉak kiḷattal
	(tol-col-kiḷa :19)
eṉṟavaḻi iṟṟeṉak kiḷatta lurimaipūṇṭa taṉṟē, ataṉaic 
ceyaṟkaip poruḷai yākkamoṭu kūṟal
	(tol-col-kiḷa:20)
eṉpuḻiyuṅ koḷḷa vaittalum,
kuṟṟiya likara niṟṟalvēṇṭum	(tol-eḻut-moḻi 1)
eṉpataṉaik, 
kuṟṟiya lukaram vallā ṟūrntu	(tol-eḻut-moḻi 3)
niṟṟal vēṇṭum eṉṟu koḷḷa vaittalum pōlvaṉa.
iṉi, vaḻakkiyalum vaḻakkiyalāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa cey yuḷiyalum paṟṟi eḻunta ilakkaṇam iyaṟṟamiḻeṉappaṭum. acceyyuḷiṉṟi amaiyāta icaiyilakkaṇam icaittami ḻeṉap peyareyti avviyaṟṟamiḻp piṉṉar vaikkap paṭṭateṉappaṭum; ivviraṇṭaṉvaḻi nikaḻttuṅ kūttilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiya nāṭakat tamiḻ avaṟṟup piṉṉarttāmeṉa muṟaimai kūṟutalum; iṉi iyaṟṟamiḻuḷḷum eḻuttatikārat tōṭu collatikārattiṟkuñ collatikārattōṭu poruḷati kārattiṟkum iyaiyu kūṟutalum atikārattuḷ ōttuppala vākaliṉ avai oṉṟaṉpiṉṉoṉṟu vaittaṟku iyaipu kūṟutalum avvāṟē cūttirattiṟku iyaipu kūṟutalu mellām atikāra muṟaimaikku iṉameṉṟu cērtti yuṇarappaṭum. piṟavum aṉṉa
atikārameṉṟa poruṇmai yeṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, muṟaimai yeṉavum, iṭameṉavuṅ, kiḻamaiyeṉavuṅ koḷḷappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ, īṇṭu atikārameṉṟoḻiyātu muṟaimai yeṉavuṅ kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ atikārameṉpatu muṟaimaip poruṭṭaṉṟi muṉ ōriṭattu niṟutti ataṉ vaḻimuṟaiyāṟ poruḷ kōṭal koḷka; eṉṉai? muṉṉum piṉṉum niṉṟa cūttiram iraṇṭaṉaiyum ōriṭattaṉavākak karutal vēṇṭum; aṅṅaṉaṅ karutaliyaipukoṇṭaṉṟi oṉṟaṉ poruṇmai oṉṟaṟku varuvittal aritākalāṉum aṅṅaṉam iṭam paṟṟākkāl oruvaṉ ceyta nūloṭu piṟatorunūṟku iyaipu kūṟātavāṟupōla eḻuttatikārattōṭu collatikārattiṭai iyaipu kūṟalvēṇṭu vataṉṟāvāṉ cellumātalāṉu meṉpatu. eṉavē, iṭameṉappaṭṭatutāṉē muṟaimaip poruḷum paṭumāyiṉum iccūttirattuḷ atikārameṉavum muṟaimaiyeṉavuṅ kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ īṇṭu iṭamuṟaimai yeṉpatē karuttāyiṟṟu. itaṉāṉē uṭaṉ piṟantāruḷ oruvaṟkuriyatu vaḻittōṉṟiṉārkkum oruvaḻi uriyavāṟupōla muṉṉar niṉṟa viti piṉṉar vantataṟkum vēṇṭiyavaḻik koḷḷappaṭumeṉpatu uttivakaiyāyiṟṟu.
iṉiyorucārār, iṅṅaṉam varaintukoṇṭa iṭattuḷḷē yāṟṟoḻukkup pōlavaṉṟi iṭaiyiṭaiyum peṟu meṉpatu nōkki arimā nōkkun, tēraip pāyttuḷum, paruntu viḻukkāṭum āki varumeṉavuñ collupa; avaiyum iṉattiṟ cērtti yuṇarttavēpaṭuvameṉpatu.
(3)	tokuttut kūṟal --- tokuttiyātta nūluḷḷun tokuttukkūṟutal :
avaiyāvaṉa : eḻuttu muppattumūṉṟu, colliraṇṭu, poruḷiraṇṭu eṉaāciriyaṉṟāṉē tokai kūṟutal pōl vaṉa
		irutiṇai maruṅki ṉaimpā laṟiya
vīṟṟiṉiṉ ṟicaikkum patiṉō reḻuttum
	(tol-col-kiḷavi : 10)
eṉap pala cūttirattu virintaṉa tokuttalum,
		āvayiṉ mūṉṟō ṭappati ṉaintum
pālaṟi vanta vuyartiṇaip peyar
	(tol-col-peya : 8)
eṉa oru cūttirattu virittut tokuttalum pōlvaṉavum ataṉ pakutiyāy aṭaṅkum.
(4)	vakuttu meynniṟuttal aṅṅaṉan tokuttukkūṟiya vaḻi eḻuttu muppattumūṉṟeṉṟa tokaiyiṉaik kuṟṟeḻuttum neṭṭeḻuttum uyirum uyirmeyyum valliṉamum melliṉamum iṭaiyiṉamu meṉṟāṟ pōlavum uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇai yeṉṟa tokaiyiṉai oruvaṉ orutti palar oṉṟu palaveṉṟāṟ pōlavum vakuttal:
		uyartiṇaik kurimaiyu maḵṟiṇaik kurimaiyu
māyiru tiṇaikkumō raṉṉa vurimaiyum
	(tol-col-peya: 6)
eṉavum,
		iyaṟco ṟirico ṟicaiccol vaṭacoleṉ
ṟaṉaittē ceyyu ḷīṭṭac collē
		(tol-col-ec: 1)
eṉavum varuvaṉavum avai ivaṟṟuḷ irutiṇaiyeṉak kūṟip peyar mūṉṟu, viṉai mūṉṟeṉṟalum; iṉi akattiṇai puṟattiṇaiyeṉakkūṟi, akampuṟameṉak kōṭalum iṉa meṉṟatu; avai kuṟṟeḻuttu neṭṭeḻutteṉa muṟkūṟi yaṉavē maṟṟoru peyar peṟṟāṟpōlātu colvakaiyāṉ mūṉṟāvatōr col vēṟu peṟṟamaiyiṉeṉpatu. iṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷākkāl irutiṇaip peyarum viṉaiyum mummūṉ ṟeṉal ākātaṉṟōveṉpatu
		colleṉap paṭupa peyarē viṉaiyeṉ
ṟāyiraṇ ṭeṉpa vaṟintici ṉōrē
	(tol.col-peya: 4)
eṉṟavaḻi,
		iṭaiccoṟ kiḷaviyu muriccoṟ kiḷaviyu
mavaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṟ ṟōṉṟu meṉpa
eṉa vakuttalum, iṉi ārāynta iyaṟcolloṭu vēṟu mūṉṟu coṟkūṭṭi nāṉkeṉṟalum avvāṟē iṉameṉap paṭum. eṉṉai? oḻinta mūṉṟu collum iyaṟcolliṉ vēṟupaṭṭamai kūṟumākali ṉeṉpatu. aḵtēl, eḻuttu muppattumūṉṟeṉak kūṟip piṉṉar irunūṟṟorupat tāṟuyir meyk kuppai kōṭalum vēṟṟeḻuttāki varu meṉpatu vakuttu meynniṟuttaleṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷāmō veṉiṉ kōṭumaṉṟē;
puḷḷi yillā vellā meyyum
eṉpuḻi,
uruvuru vāki yakaramo ṭuyirttalum
eṉa āciriyaṉ iraṇṭeḻuttiṉ kūṭṭamākac cūttirañ ceytilāṉāyiṉeṉpatu.
meyyeṉṟataṉāṉ vakuttavaṟṟaip piṉṉum vakuttu niṟuttal koḷḷappaṭum; eṉṉai? iyaṟcīrpatteṉavum āciriyavuriccīrāṟeṉavum vakuttavaṟṟai,
		iyaṟcī riṟuti nērava ṇiṟpi
ṉuriccīr veṇpā vāku meṉpa	(tol.cey: 19)
eṉa iyalacaimayakkamākiya nāṉkaṉaiyē iyaṟcīreṉavum,
		veṇpā vuriccī rāciriya vuriccī
riṉpā nēraṭik koruṅkunilai yilavē
	(tol-cey: 23)
eṉṟavaḻi, āciriyavuriccīr āṟaṉuḷ iraṇṭaṉai oḻittu uriyacai mayakkamākiya nāṉkaṉaiyē āciriyavuriccīreṉavum vakuttu niṉṟamaiyiṉeṉpatu.
mey yeṉṟataṉāṉ vakuttavaṟṟaip piṉṉum vakuttu niṟuttal koḷḷappaṭum; eṉṉai?
		iyalacai mayakka miyaṟcī rēṉai
yuriyacai mayakka māciriya vuriccīr	(tol-cey : 13)
eṉṟu pakuttōtiyavāṟṟāṉeṉpatu; akkaruttiṉāṉaṉṟē oḻinta iraṇṭaṉaiyuṅ,
kalittaḷai maruṅkiṟ kaṭiyavum paṭāa
	(tol-cey : 24)
eṉa iṟantatutaḻīi uraippāṉāyiṟṟeṉpatu.
(5)	moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟa vavvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉai muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittal --- eṭuttōtiya poruṇmaikku ēṟṟa vakaiyāṉ apporuṇmaikkaṭ  collātatoṉṟu koḷḷavaittal.
muṭṭiṉṟi muṭittaleṉṟataṉāṉ eṭuttōtātum eṭuttōtiyataṉōṭu okkuñ ciṟappiṟṟeṉṟavāṟām. atu pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum maṟṟu itaṉai aruttāpatti yeṉṉāmōveṉiṉ, eṉṉāmaṉṟē :
piṟacī ruḷvaḻit taṉṟaḷai vēṇṭupa
eṉṉum poruṭṭaṉṟit taṉcīroṭu iyaṟcīr vantu taḷai koḷḷumeṉa moḻiyātōr poruḷkōṭaliṉeṉka,
iṉi,
raḵkā ṉoṟṟum pakara viṟutiyum	(tol-col : 7)
eṉṟavaḻi oḻinta nāṉkeḻuttum īṟṟiṉiṟkumeṉṟu kōṭal pōlvaṉavum ataṉ iṉameṉappaṭum. eṉṉai? oṉṟeṉa muṭitta ṟaṉṉiṉa muṭitta leṉiṉum iḻukkātu. iṉic,
cīriyai maruṅki ṉōracai yoppiṉ
	(tol-col : 56)
eṉṟavaḻi, oṉṟātatu iyaṟcīrveṇṭaḷai yeṉṟukōṭal aruttāpattiyāki,
eṭutta moḻiyiṉañ ceppalu murittu
	(tol-cey : 61)
eṉṟa vaḻakkiyalāṉē vaḻuvaṉṟi aṭaṅkumeṉpatu.
(6)	vārātataṉāṉ vantatu muṭittal---oru poruṇ maikku vēṇṭum ilakkaṇam nirampa vārātatōr cūttirat tāṉē aṅṅaṉam vanta poruṇmaikku vēṇṭum muṭipu koḷḷacceytal; atu,
		toṭara liṟuti tammuṟ ṟāmvariṉ
lakaram ṟakaravoṟ ṟākalu murittē
	(tol-eḻut-uyir : 12)
eṉa ataṟkuk kēṭu vārāta cūttirattāṉē lakaram ṟakara voṟṟāy varumeṉap pirittut tiripu kūṟiyatē paṟṟāka lakaram āṇṭu nillātu keṭumeṉṟu koḷḷavaittataṉāṉē ciṟcila vittip paṟpala koṇṭāreṉṟu vanta puṇarcci muṭittavāṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.
		ellā meṉṉu miṟuti muṉṉar
vaṟṟeṉ cāriyai muṟṟat tōṉṟum
	(tol-eḻut-uru : 17)
eṉṟavaḻi, ellāmeṉṉum viravuppeyaruḷ uyartiṇai kūṟivaṟṟuccāriyai peṟāmaiyāṉ atu peṟṟuvanta aḵṟiṇaik kūṟē muṭittalum atu; piṟavum aṉṉa.
iṉi, iṅṅaṉam muṭipukōṭalaṉṟi āṇṭuk kuṟiyiṭutalum āṭciyuṅ kuṟiyīṭum oruṅkunikaḻntatu piṉṉar āṭcik kaṇ vārāmaiyum vantavaḻip piṟavaṟṟōṭu kūṟutalum ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. avai,
avvakai yoṉpatum viṉaiyeñcu kiḷavi
	(tol-col-viṉai : 31)
eṉa muṭintataṉai mīṇṭum,
neṟippaṭat tōṉṟu meñcuporuṭ kiḷavi
	(tol-col-ecca : 34)
eṉak kuṟiyiṭutal eccaviyaluḷ vantatāyiṉum muṉṉar ak kuṟiyīṭu vantatiṉmaiyiṉ atu vārātateṉappaṭumākaliṉ aḵtu īṇṭuk kuṟiyiṭutalāyiṟṟu. viṉaiyiyaluṭ peyarecca meṉṟu āḷutalum atu.
vaṇṇac ciṉaiccol muṟṟuccol eṉpaṉavuṅ kuṟṟiyalikarattaip puḷḷiyeṉṟalum āṭciyuṅ kuṟiyīṭum oruṅku nikaḻntaṉavākaliṉ avaiyum iṉi vārāmaiyāṉ vantuḻi  vantuḻi avvāṟu āṇṭāṉeṉpatu.
iṉip, puḷḷiyeṉa mēl āḷavārātataṉaip puḷḷi yeṉṟu āḷvaṉavaṟṟoṭu mayakkaṅ kūṟutaleṉpatu,
		avaitāṅ,
kuṟṟiya vikaraṅ kuṟṟiya lukara
māyta meṉṟu
muppāṟ puḷḷiyu meḻuttō raṉṉa
	(tol-eḻut-nūṉ : 2)
eṉpuḻik kuṟṟiyavikaram puḷḷiyeṉṟu yāṇṭum āḷavārā maiyālum atutāṉ avvaḻi varavēṇṭutalāṉum aṅṅaṉam puḷḷiyeṉṟu āḷavaruṅ kuṟṟukarattōṭum āytattōṭum uṭaṉkūṟutalāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam uṭaṉkūṟākkāṟ puḷḷiyuṅ kuṟṟikaramumeṉac cūttirampeṟutal vēṇṭuvatāvāṉ cellu meṉpatu; iṉi
ā ē ō ammūṉṟum viṉā	(tol-eḻut-nūṉ: 32)
eṉavum,
māṟuko ḷeccamum viṉāvu maiyamum
	(tol-eḻut-uyir: 88)
eṉavuṅ kūṟuvaṉavum avai. eṉṉai? iṭaiccollōt tiṉuḷ viṉāveṉṟōtāta ākāram vārātatuṭaṉ āṇṭu viṉāveṉṟu ōtiya ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ
ā ē ō ammūṉṟum viṉā
eṉṟu uṭaṉ kūṟiṉamaiyāṉum, avvāṟu iṭaiccol lōttiṉuḷ eṭuttōtāta māṟukoḷeccattoṭum aiyat tōṭum āṇṭōtiya viṉāviṉaiyum eṇṇiṉaiyum eḻuttōttiṉuḷ vitantuṭaṉ kūṟiṉamaiyāṉumeṉpatu; itu nōkkip pōlum.
īṟṟacai yivvain tēkāram	(tol-col-iṭai: 9)
eṉa iṭaiccolliṉai eḻuttuccāriyai peytōtiya karut tāṉē ipporuṇmaikoḷḷa vaippāṉāyiṟṟu meṉpatu.
maṟṟu ivai etiratupōṟṟalākāvōveṉiṉ,--- atu poruṭpaṭaik kaṇṇateṉavum ivai āṭciyuṅ kuṟiyīṭum paṟṟiyatōr pakuti yeṉavum kūṟi viṭukka. aḵtēṟ, kuṟi yīṭṭāl īṇṭārāyāṉōveṉiṉ,--- ivai uttivakai yākalāṉum atu tāṉē uttiyeṉap paṭumātalāṉum ataṉai īṇṭu ārāyāṉeṉpatu.
(7)	vantatukoṇṭu vārātatuṇarttal --- piṉṉoru vaḻi vantatukoṇṭu muṉvārātatōr poruḷ aṟiya vaittal; atu,
ñāṅkark kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikum
	(tol.eḻut-uyir: 2)
eṉpatu.
		eñciya mūṉṟu mēlvantu muṭikku
meñcuporuṭ kiḷavi yilaveṉa moḻipa
	(tol.eḻut-ecca: 43)
eṉa, vantatu koṇṭu mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa ēḻeccattiṟkum mēl vantu muṭikkuñ col vārātataṉai varumeṉṟuṇarntu koḷḷavaittamaiyiṉ ituvum atuvēyāyiṟṟu.
āyiru tiṇaiyi ṉicaikkumaṉa collē
	(tol-col-kiḷa : 1)
eṉṟavaḻit tiṇaiyeṉṉum peyar epporuṭkum eytuvittal oru cūttirattuḷḷē kōṭaliṉ ataṉai ataṟku iṉameṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum; piṟavum aṉṉa.
(8)	muntu moḻintataṉ ṟalaitaṭumāṟṟu---muṉṉoru kāṟ kūṟiya muṟaiyaṉṟip piṉṉorukāl talai taṭumāṟākak kūṟutal; atu,
paṉṉī ruyirumoḻimuta lākum
	(tol-eḻut-moḻi : 26)
eṉavum,
uyirmey yallaṉa moḻimuta lākā
	(tol-eḻut-moḻi : 27)
eṉavum, uyirum meyyum niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉaṉṟik,
		katana pamaveṉu māvain teḻuttu
mellā vuyiroṭuñ cellumār
	(tol-eḻut-moḻi : 28)
eṉavum meypaṟṟi varaiyaṟai kūṟutalum,
		ellā moḻikku miṟutiyu mutalu
meyyē yuyireṉ ṟāyī riyala	(tol-eḻut-puṇa : 1)
eṉa muṟkūṟiyamuṟai piṟaḻak kūṟutalumāyiṉa.
meyyum uyirum paṟṟi vitantu varaiyaṟuppiṉum aḵtu iraṇṭaṟkuñ cellumeṉṟu kōṭaṟkum, iṉi iyalpuvakai yāṉ īṟāka orutalaiyāka uṭaiyaṉa meyyeṉṟaṟkum avvāṟu kūṟiṉāṉeṉṉāmōveṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉamē ak karuttiṉāṉaṉṟē ottakāṭciyeṉak kuṟṟattōṭu oppumai kūṟi paṟṟup poruḷ payattaliṉ amaiyumeṉṟu koḷvāmā yiṟṟeṉpatu.
iṉi,
mūṉṟu talaiyiṭṭa muppatiṟ ṟeḻuttiṉ
	(tol-eḻut - puṇa : 1)
eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa iṉa meṉappaṭum.
puḷḷi yillā vellā meyyum
	(tol-eḻut-nūṉ : 17)
eṉpatu mey muṟkūṟiṉamaiyiṉ ituvum itaṟku utāraṇa meṉappaṭum.
maṟṟu, mayakkaṅkūṟalōṭu itaṉiṭai vēṟṟumaiyeṉṉai yeṉiṉ, --- aḵtu
iṉṉē vaṟṟē	(tol-eḻut-puṇa : 17)
eṉa niṟuttamuṟaiyāṟ kūṟātu maṟṟatuvē paṟṟāka maṟṟoru poruḷ koḷḷappaṭum. iḵtu iṉṉataṉṟi iṅṅaṉam mayaṅkak kūṟalvēṇṭum. poruṇmaittāki varumeṉpatu; aḵtēl, itu māṟukoḷakkūṟaleṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamākāvō veṉiṉ, --- ākātu; eṉṉai? itu muṟkūṟiya poruḷai māṟupaṭāmai yāṉum niṟuttamuṟai talaitaṭumāṟa vaikkun tuṇaiyākalāṉu meṉpatu.
(9)	oppakkūṟal : oṉṟu kūṟuṅkāl iruporuṭ kuṟitta teṉṟu iraṭṭuṟacceytal. atu,
		iṉṉi ṉikara māvi ṉiṟuti
muṉṉark keṭuta lurittu mākum
	(tol-eḻut-puṇa : 18)
eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa.
viṉaiyeñcu kiḷaviyu muvamak kiḷaviyum,
	(tol-eḻut-uyir : 2)
eṉavum,
aṉṉa veṉṉu muvamak kiḷaviyum
	(tol-eḻut-uyar : 8)
eṉavum iṉamallaṉavaṟṟai uṭaṉeṇṇutalum,
māmarak kiḷaviyu māvu māvum
	(tol-eḻut-uyir, 29)
eṉa māṭṭeṟiyuṅkāl vēṟuvēṟu vitiyuṭaiyaṉavaṟṟai oruṅku māṭṭeṟitalum, āṇṭu āṟu valleḻuttiṉaiyum uṭaṉ kōṭalum, nilaimoḻittoḻiloṭu varumoḻit toḻilum oppukkoṇṭu māṭṭeṟitalum pōlvaṉa oppakkūṟaleṉṉum pakutiyāy aṭaṅkumeṉpatu.
(10)	orutalai moḻital---ōr atikārattiṟ collaṟ pālataṉai vēṟu atikārattuc colli avvilakkaṇamē āṇṭuṅ koḷḷavaittal; atu,
a i u ammūṉṟum cuṭṭu	(tol-eḻut-nūṉ : 31)
eṉavum,
āṇum peṇṇu maḵṟiṇai yiyaṟkai
	(tol-eḻut-puḷḷi : 8)
eṉavum ivai eḻuttatikārattuk kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉē collati kārattuḷḷum avvilakkaṇaṅ koḷḷa vaittamaiyiṉ appeyarttāyiṟṟu.
uṭampoṭupuṇarttuc colluvaṉa ataṟku iṉa meṉappaṭum. eṉṉai? vitiyallātatu vitipōla maṟṟoru vaḻic cēṟaliṉ.
(11)	taṉkōṭkūṟal---collātaṉa piṟavuḷavāyiṉum annūṟku vēṇṭuvatē koḷvaleṉṟal; atu,
		aḵtiva ṇuvalā teḻuntupuṟat ticaikku
meyteri vaḷiyicai yaḷavunuvaṉ ṟiciṉē
	(tol-eḻut-piṟap : 20)
eṉavum,
		cakarak kiḷaviyu mavaṟṟō raṟṟē
aaiauveṉu mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē
	(tol-eḻut-moḻi : 29)
eṉavum,
kuṟṟeḻut taintu moḻiniṟai pilavē
	(tol-eḻut-moḻi : 11)
eṉavum,
pāṭaluṭ payiṉṟavai nāṭuṅ kālai
	(tol-akat : 3)
eṉavum varum.
ivvāṟṟāṉē.
aḷapiṟ kōṭa lantaṇar maṟaittu
	(tol-eḻut-piṟap : 20)
āyiṉum atu kūṟiṉēṉeṉavum, iyaṟcolliṟkallatu nilai moḻiyākkaṅ kūṟēṉeṉavum,
vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum ārāyval
eṉṟu pukuntāṉ pāṭaluṭ payiṉṟa vaḻakkē kūṟuvaleṉavuṅ kūṟutaliṉ avai taṉkōṭkūṟutalām.
iṉi, oṉpatu mayakkattuṇ meymmayakkaṅkūṟi oḻintaṉa kūṟāmaiyum viṉaittokaiyum paṇputtokaiyum eṭuttōti muṭiyāmaiyum,
		nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉu mulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum
pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam
	(tol-akat : 53)
paṟṟip poruḷilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvaleṉṟalum, perumpāṉmai ilakkaṇa vaḻakkeṉpa eṉṟalum, ataṉāṉē ciṟupāṉmaiyai mayakkameṉṟalum, am ām em ēm eṉpaṉa mutalāyavaṟṟai aṅṅaṉam pakuttōtutaṟpayaṉum, avai viṉaiyiṉṟi av viṉai ceytāṉmēl nikaḻkiṉṟa kūṟātalumē paṟṟi,
viṉaiceyal maruṅkiṟ kālamoṭu varunavum
	(tol-col-iṭai : 2)
eṉṟu iṭaiccolloṭu ōtutalum pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum; eṉṉai? ivaitāṅ kūṟuvaleṉṟu pukunta vaṟṟuḷḷum oru poruḷāṉavaṟṟai varaintu koṇṭamaiyiṉ avaṟṟuḷ viriyeṉappaṭṭaṉa.
(12)	muṟaipiṟaḻāmai --- kāraṇamiṉṟit tāṉ cila poruḷ eṇṇi niṟuttiyapiṉṉar ammuṟai piṟaḻntāluṅ kuṟṟamil vaḻiyum am muṟaiyiṉaiyē ilakkaṇamākac collutal atu.
		peyar ai oṭu ku
iṉ atu kaṇviḷi yeṉṉu mīṟṟa
	(tol-col-vēṟ : 3)
eṉa niṟuttamuṟaiyāṟpaṟṟi eḻuvāyvēṟṟumai iraṇṭāvatu mūṉṟāvateṉap peyarkoṭuttal.
akaramuta ṉakara viṟuvāy	(tol-eḻut-nūṉ : 1)
eṉavuṅ,
kaikkiḷai mutalāp peruntiṇai yiṟuvāy
	(tol-akat : 1)
eṉavum, vaḻakkiyalāṉum ilakkaṇa vakaiyāṉum uḷporuḷai vitantē eṇṇi niṟuttāta vaḻiyum,
		avaṟṟuḷ,
a i u e ō
eṉavum,
		avaṟṟuḷ
naṭuvaṇaintiṇai naṭuvaṇa toḻiya	(tol-akat: 2)
eṉavum muṟaipiṟaḻāmaṟ kōṭal ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
(13)	piṟaṉuṭampaṭṭatu tāṉuṭampaṭutal---uḷ poruḷ aṉṟāyiṉum vaḻakkiyalāṟ koḷporuḷ ituveṉak kūṟutal; atu,
paṇṭiyaṉ maruṅkiṉmarī iyamarapu
	(tol-col-vēṟ.ma: 7)
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavaṟṟāṉ aṟika.
iṉi,
mīyeṉa marīiya viṭamvarai kiḷaviyum
	(tol-eḻut-uyir : 48)
eṉpuḻi mēleṉpatu ilakkaṇameṉṟu eṭuttōtiyataṉai marūuveṉṟamaiyiṉ atuvum ataṉpāṟpaṭum. eṉṉai? mutaṉūluṭkoṇṭavāṟaṟintu maṟṟu ataṉaittāṉ iccol iṉṉavāṟāyiṟṟeṉṟu ilakkaṇaṅkūṟātu uṭampaṭutaliṉ. maṟṟu ataṉai iṉameṉpateṟṟukku? itutāṉē piṟaṉuṭam paṭṭatu tāṉuṭampaṭṭatākātō mutaṉūlāciriyaṉ uṭampaṭṭatākaliṉeṉiṉ---aṟṟaṉṟu; mutaṉūlāciriyaṉaip piṟaṉeṉṉāmaiyāṉum, mutaṉūliṉ vaḻittākiya nūluḷ avaṉ uṭampaṭṭa toṉṟu uṭampaṭumeṉṟu uttivakaiyāṟ koḷḷātu muḻuvatūuṅ koḷḷumākalāṉum avvāypāṭu kūṟalākāteṉpatu.
iṉi, mutaṉūluḷ mēleṉpatu mīyeṉa marīiyiṟṟeṉṟu vitantōtappaṭṭataṉai aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟātu mīyeṉa marīiyiṟ ṟeṉṟu vāḷātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, ataṉai iṉameṉṟu koṇ ṭāmeṉpatu; eṉavē, īṇṭup piṟaṉeṉṟatu vaḻakkiṉuḷ ḷōrai nōkkiyāyiṟṟu; eṉṟāṟku oḻinta vaḻinūlāciri yaraip piṟaṉeṉṟāṉeṉṉāmōveṉiṉ, avaruṭampaṭṭatu uṭampaṭṭataṉāṟ payantateṉṉai? mutaṉūliṟ piṟaḻāmai nūlceyyumāyiṉeṉa maṟukka.
allatūum icainūluṅ kūttanūlumpaṟṟip piṟaṉ kōṭ kūṟaleṉpataṉāṟ piṟaṉeṉṉiṉaṉṟi iyaṟṟamiḻk kaṇṇē mutaṉūlāciriyaṉait piṟaṉeṉṉāṉeṉpatu ; aḵtēl vaḻakkunūl, ceyvāṉ avvaḻakkiṉai vaḻaṅku vāraip piṟaṉeṉṇumōveṉiṉ, ilakkaṇamum vaḻakkumeṉa iraṇṭaṉuḷ iḵtilakkaṇamātaliṉ avvaḻakkiṉuḷ vaḻaṅku vāraip piṟaṉeṉṟāl amaiyumaṉṟōveṉpatu ; eṉṟāṟku avaruṭampaṭṭatu uṭampaṭutal uttivakaiyeṉpa teṟṟukku? aḵtu iyalpēyaṉṟōveṉiṉ,- aṅṅaṉam --- marīiyiṉum ilakkaṇameṉpatu tiripillātākaliṟ ṟiripupaṭum vaḻakkiṉai uṭampaṭutal ilakkaṇa mēyāmeṉpatu karuttu ; allākkāl eḷḷēpōla eṭkup paiyun taṉ taṉmaiyāṉ uḷ poruḷākalum vēṇṭu maṉṟē veṉpatu.
(14)	iṟantatu kāttal---eṉpatu, muṟkūṟiyavōr cūttirap poruṇmaiyaip piṉṉoru cūttirattāṉ vilakkutal)
atu,
pālkeḻu kiḷavi nālvarkku murittē	(tol-poru:5)
eṉak kūṟiya piṉṉar,
naṭpi ṉaṭakkai yāṅkalaṅ kaṭaiyē (tol-poru: 6)
eṉṟāṟpōla vilakkutal,
nūṟpuṟaṉaṭaiyum ōttuppuṟaṉaṭaiyum atikārap puṟaṉaṭaiyum pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. avai,
		īṟiyaṉ maruṅki ṉivaiyivaṟ ṟiyalpeṉak
kūṟiya kiḷavip pallā ṟellā
meyttalaip paṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi
yottavai yuriya puṇarmoḻi nilaiyē
	(tol-eḻut-tokai : 29)
eṉavum,
		puḷḷi yiṟutiyu muyiriṟu kiḷaviyuñ
colliya valla vēṉaiya vellām
	(tol-eḻut-uru : 30)
eṉavum varuvaṉapōlvaṉa.
(15)	etiratu pōṟṟal---varukiṉṟa cūttirapporuṇmaik kēṟpa vēṟoruporuṇ muṟkūṟutal; atu,
īṟāku puḷḷi yakaramoṭu nilaiyum
	(tol-eḻut-tokai : 19)
eṉa varukiṉṟataṉai nōkki,
		āṟa ṉurupi ṉakarak kiḷavi
yīṟā kakaramuṉaik keṭutal vēṇṭum
	(tol-eḻut-puṇa : 18)
eṉak kūṟutalum,
pallōr paṭarkkai muṉṉilai taṉmai
	(tol-col-viṉai : 30)
eṉa varukiṉṟataṉai nēkkit,
		taṉmaic collē yaḵṟiṇaik kiḷaviyeṉ
ṟeṇṇuvaḻi maruṅkiṉ viravutal varaiyār
	(tol-col-kiḷavi : 43)
eṉa vaḻuvamaittalum pōlvaṉa.
iṉi, oru cūttirattuḷḷē,
īṟāku puḷḷi yakaramoṭu nilaiyum
	(tol-eḻut-tokai : 19)
eṉa varuvataṉai nōkkik kūṟiya,
kuṟṟoṟ ṟiraṭṭa lillai
	tol-eḻut-tokai :19)
eṉṟal ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
(16)	moḻivāmeṉṟal---oru payaṉōkki muṟkūṟutu meṉṟal; avai,

	(tol-eḻut-mo :2)
eṉavum,

	(tol-eḻut-moḻi :4)

		avvē,
ivveṉa vaṟitaṟku meypeṟak kiḷappa
	(tol-col.viḷi :2)
eṉavum varum.
iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiyataṉāṟ payaṉ : kuṟṟukara vīṟṟukkaṇaṉṟip puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟikara miṉṟeṉpatūum, iṉip puṇar moḻik kuṟṟikaram peruvaraviṟṟeṉpatūum aṟivitta lāyiṟṟu.
		avvē,
ivveṉa vaṟitaṟku meypeṟak kiḷappa
eṉpatūum,
* avviḷiyēlā eṉṟatūum avviḷiyilakkaṇa meṉṟaṟ payam paṭa vantatu.
īṟṟup potuviṉāl viḷiyēṟkumeṉṟu ōtappaṭṭa peyaruḷ ivai viḷiyēlāveṉa viḷivilakkal vēṇṭutalāṉum, nī vārāyeṉpatu iyalpuviḷiyaṉṟeṉa vilakkutalum viḷiyilakkaṇamē yātalāṉu meṉpatu.
iṉi,
		meypeṟak kiḷantu poruḷvarain ticaikku
maikāra vēṟṟumait tiripeṉa moḻipa
eṉavum,
		melveḻut tiyaṟkai colliya muṟaiyāṉ
ṅañanama veṉṉu moṟṟā kummē
	(tol-eḻut- tokai :1)

(17)	kūṟiṟṟeṉṟal---muṟkūṟiyatōr ilakkaṇattiṉai maṟṟoru poruṭkum vitikkavēṇṭiya vaḻi, avvilakkaṇat tiṉai mīṭṭuṅ kūṟātu mēṟkūṟiyavāṟṟāṉē koḷka veṉpāṉ avai kūṟiṉāmeṉṟu nekiḻntu pōtal avai,

(19)	orutalaiyaṉmai---

	(tol-eḻut-uyir : 33)

atu,
		kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāytap puḷḷi
yuyiroṭu puṇarntavaṉ lāṟaṉ micaittē
	(tol-eḻut- moḻi :5)
eṉṟakkāl yāṇṭum orutalaiyāka vārātu, varuñāṉṟu varuvatu āṇṭeṉṟu koḷḷavaittal,
iṉic, collōttiṉuḷ vēṟṟumaiyeṉṟu ōtappaṭṭa eṭṭaṉuḷ eḻuvāyvēṟṟumaiyiṉaiyum viḷi vēṟṟumaiyiṉaiyum vēṟṟumaiyeṉṉātu eḻuttōttiṉuḷ alvaḻiyeṉṟal pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
(20)	muṭintatu kāṭṭal --- collukiṉṟa poruṭku vēṇṭuvaṉavellāñ collātu tollāciriyar kūṟiṉā reṉṟu collutal.
aḵtu oṉṟaṟivatu uṟṟaṟiva teṉṟaṟkum, iraṇṭaṟivatu uṟṟuñ cuvaittum aṟivatu eṉṟaṟkum muṟaimaiyāṟ cūttirañ ceyvāṉ avai avvāṟātaṟkuk kāraṇaṅ kūṟātu,
nēriti ṉuṇarntōr neṟippaṭut tiṉarē
	(tol-mara :27)
eṉa muṭintatu kāṭṭal.
nuṇṇiti ṉuṇarntōr kaṇṭavāṟē
	(tol-eḻut-nūṉ :7)
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
(21)	āṇai kūṟal---ivvāciriyaṉ karuttu ituveṉak koḷḷa vaittal :atu,
		ammi ṉiṟuti kacatak kālait
taṉmey tirintu ṅañana vākum
	(tol-eḻut-puṇa : 27)
eṉat karuviyōttiṉuṭ cāriyai makaram pakaravarumoḻik kaṇ tiriyāteṉṟu pōyc ceykaiyuḷ valveḻuttiṉatu iyaṟkai melleḻuttātal aṟivittaṟku alvaḻi yellā melleḻuttāku meṉac collutal pōlvaṉa. ṅañana ātaṟkum iḵtokkum.
		ōmpaṭai yāṇaiyiṟ kiḷantavaṟ ṟiyalāṟ
pāṅkuṟa vuṇarta leṉmaṉār pulavar
	(tol-col-uric 98)
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
maṟṟut taṉkōṭkūṟalōṭu itaṉiṭai vēṟṟumai yeṉṉai yeṉiṉ, atu tantirañceyyum pakutikkaṇṇatu; iḵtu aṉṉataṉṟip puṇarccikkaṭ ciṟappuṭaiya nilaimoḻi varumoḻikkut tiripupōlāteṉṟu karuviyākiya iṭaic coṟkāyiṉ ittuṇai yamaiyumeṉṟu āṇai ceytaliṉ appeyarttāyiṟṟu.
(22)	palporuṭ kēṟpiṉ nallatu kōṭal --- oru cūttirattuṭ payanta coṟṟoṭar palaporuṭ kēṟṟatāyiṉum nallatu koḷkiṉṟāreṉak karuti avvāṟu ceytal: avai,
		oruva reṉṉum peyarnilaik kiḷavi
yirupāṟku murittē teriyuṅ kālai
	(tol-col-peya :37)
eṉṟavaḻi, oruvareṉpatoru col taṉkaṇṇē iru pālāraiyun taḻīi niṟkumeṉavum, atu karuviyāka iru pālāraiyuñ collappaṭumeṉavum kavarntavaḻi,
taṉmai cuṭṭiṟ paṉmaik kēṟkum
	(tol-col-peya :38)
eṉpataṉōṭu paṭuttunōkka irupālāraiyum orucol taṉkaṭṭaḻīi niṟṟalē nallateṉṟu koḷḷavaittal.
numme ṉiṟuti yiyaṟkai yākum
	(tol-eḻut- uru :15)
eṉpuḻi, eḻuttu vikāramuṭaiyataṉaik kaḷaintu cāriyaik kaṇṇē iyaṟkai kōṭalum ataṉpāṟpaṭum. itaṉai ēṟpuḻik kōṭaleṉavum orupuṭaic cēṟaleṉavuñ collupa.
(23)	tokuttamoḻiyāṉ vakuttaṉar kōṭal---oru vāypāṭu eṭuttōtap palavāypāṭu ataṟku vantu pūṇumeṉṟu vakuttuk koḷḷavaittal :atu,

purattal puṟantara lōmputal pōṟṟal
eṉap palavāka vakuttaluṅ kaṇṭukoḷka.

(24)	maṟutalaicitaittut taṉṟuṇipuraittal---oru poruḷiṉai oruvaṉ vēṟupaṭakkoḷvatōr uṇarvu tōṉṟiyakkāl av vēṟupāṭṭiṉai māṟṟit tāṉ tuṇintavāṟu avaṟkum aṟivuṟuttal. itu maṟutalai citaittaluṭaimaiyiṉ vāḷātu taṉkōṭ kūṟaliṉ aṭaṅkātāyiṟṟu.

(26)	aṟiyā tuṭampaṭal --- tāṉōtiya ilakkaṇattiṉ  vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉa tāṉ aṟintilāṉākak kūṟi ataṉpuṟattuc ceyvatoru puṟaṉaṭai; avai

(27)	poruḷiṭaiyiṭutal --- vēṟṟumaip poruḷiṉaic colkiṉṟa poruṇmaikkiṭaiyē peytucollutalum, colkiṉṟa poruṭku iyaipuṭaiyataṉai āṇṭuc collātu iṭaiyiṭṭuppōyp piṟitoruvaḻic collutalum pōlvaṉa avai :

avaiyal kiḷavi maṟaittaṉar kiḷattal
	(tol-col-ec :46)
eṉac collutalum pōlvaṉa.
		tāṉeṉ peyaruñ cuṭṭumutaṟ peyarum
yāṉeṉ peyarum viṉāviṉ peyaru
maṉṟi yaṉaittum viḷikō ḷilavē
	(tol-col-viḷi :20)
eṉṟavaḻit, tāṉeṉṉum viravup peyariṉai iṭaippeytu vilakkutalum vēṟṟumaik kiḷaviyōttiṉuṭ kūṟātu eñciniṉṟa vēṟṟumait tokai mutaliya eccaṅkaḷai eccaviyaluṭ collutalum pōlvaṉavum ataṉpāṟ cārttiyuṇarka.
(28)	etirporu ḷuṇarttal---tāṉ kūṟiya ilak kaṇattiṟ cila piṟkālattut tiripupaṭiṉum paṭumeṉpatu, mutaṟ kāla mutaṉūluṅ koṇṭuṇarnta āciriyaṉ etirkālattu varuvatu nōkki ataṟkēṟṟatōr ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟippōtal :
		makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varainta
ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi yoṉpaḵ teṉpa
pukaṟak kiḷanta vaḵṟiṇai mēṉa
	tol-eḻut-moḻi : 49)
eṉṟu ōtiya ilakkaṇattuc cila piṟkālattuk kuṟaiyavarutal etirporuḷeṉappaṭum. ataṉait tāṉ uṇarntu.
kaṭico lillaik kālattup paṭiṉē
	(tol-col-ec : 26)
eṉṟu kūṟavē muṟkūṟiya poruḷiṉai vaṟpuṟuttalām. atu veṉpatu. iṅṅaṉan tiripupaṭutal aṟintē kūṟutalāṉum etir poruḷākalāṉum iḵtaṟiyātu uṭampaṭutaliṉaṭaṅkātāyiṟṟu.
		poyyum vaḻuvun tōṉṟiya piṉṉa
raiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam	(tol-kaṟ : 4)

eṉpuḻi, ellā meṉpataṉai eccappaṭuttaṟkākātaṉa irupat tāṟu koṇṭa vaḻiyum ataṉai eṭuttōtiṟ ciṟappiṉṟeṉṟu koḷḷaṟka eṉpatu itaṉ karuttu.
eccaviyaluṭ kūṟiya poruḷ akattōttuk kūṟiya poruḷōṭu oppak kūṟutalum ataṉpāṟpaṭum.
moḻinta poruḷōṭoṉṟa vavvayiṉ moḻi yātataṉai muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittaloṭu itaṉiṭai vēṟṟumai yeṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, aḵtu eṭuttōtappaṭṭa poruṭkaṇṇa teṉavum, iḵtu eṭuttōttiṉōṭu oppa eccappaṭa vaittuk koḷḷum ilakkaṇam eṉavum ataṉō ṭitaṉiṭai vēṟṟumai yuṇarka.

		neṭṭeḻut timparum toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟuṅ
kuṟṟiya lukaram tllāùrntē(tol-eḻut-moḻi: 3)vd iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum kūṟi ataṉāṉ īṟu ākkaṅ kōṭaluṅ,
		kuṟṟiya lukara muṟaippeyar maruṅki
ṉoṟṟiya nakaramicai nakaramoṭu mutalum
	(tol-eḻut-moḻi : 34)
eṉa mutalākkaṅ kūṟiyataṉāṉē iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum atuvē yeṉak kōṭalum pōlvaṉa.
peyarviṉaikku ōtiya ilakkaṇam oḻinta coṟkuñ cevvāṉañ cellumeṉṟu kōṭal ataṟku iṉameṉap paṭum piṟavum aṉṉa.
maṟṟu nutaliyataṟitaloṭu itaṉiṭai vēṟṟumai yeṉṉai yeṉiṉ, ---atu vāḷātu payamila kūṟiyatupōlak kūṟiyavaḻi ivvāṟu kūṟiyatu iṉṉa karuttup pōlu meṉṟu aṟiyavaittalum, uraivakaiyāṉum nutaliyataṟiyac collutalumām; iḵtu aṉṉataṉṟi accūttiran taṉṉāṉ oruporuḷ payantataṉṟalaiyum piṉṉoruporuḷ peṟa varutaliṉ ituvēṟeṉpatu.
maṟṟu ñāpakaṅ kūṟaloṭu itaṉiṭai vēṟṟumai yeṉṉai yeṉiṉ,---payamillatu pōlavum aritum peritumākavum iyaṟṟi eḷituñ ciṟitumāka iyaṟṟātu cūttirañ ceytal vēṟupāṭē nimittamākat tōṉṟik koḷvatoru poruḷ peṟa vaittaliṉ ituvum vēṟeṉappaṭumeṉpatu.
meyppaṭa nāṭic colliya allapiṟa avaṇ variṉum --- uyttukkoṇṭuṇartaloṭu mēṟkūṟiya muppat tiraṇṭum iccūttirattuḷ eṭuttōtiya poruḷvakaiyāṉ ārāyntu collappaṭṭaṉavaṉṟē? aṅṅaṉañ collātaṉa piṟavum innūluḷ variṉum; colliyavakaiyāṟ curuṅka nāṭi---uttivakaiyeṉa vakuttukkoṇṭu ōtiya muppattiraṇṭu pakutiyāṉ aṭaṅkumāṟu ārāyntu; maṉattiṉ eṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇṭu---ōtappaṭṭa utti palavum oruṅkuvariṉum uḷḷattāl teḷḷitiṉ ārāyntu mayakkantīra vēṟuvēṟu terintu vāṅkikkoṇṭu; iṉattil cērtti uṇarttal vēṇṭum---muppattiraṇṭākum ēṟṟavakaiyāṉ iṉañcārtti maṟṟavaṟṟai iṉṉatituveṉap peyar kūṟal vēṇṭum; aṅṅaṉan tokaniṉṟa vaḻiyum vēṟuvēṟu koṇṭu; nuṉittaku pulavar kūṟiya nūlē---talaimai cāṉṟa āciriyarāṟ kūṟappaṭṭa nūl (e---ṟu),
eṇṇiya muppattiraṇṭumallaṉa tōṉṟiṉum avaṟṟuḷ aṭakki, avaitām oruṅkuvariṉum vēṟu terintu iṉan tōṟuñ cērttutalai avāvi niṟkum īṇṭu ōtiya nūleṉpatu karuttu.
colliya alla piṟa avaṇ varumāṟum, avai colliyavakaiyāṟ curuṅka nāṭi iṉattiṟ cērttumāṟum mēṟ kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
iṉi, oruṅku palavutti vantavaḻi uḷḷattāl teḷḷiti ṉeṇṇitterintu koṇṭu iṉattiṟ cērttutal varumāṟu:
		aṉṉapiṟavuṅ kiḷanta valla
paṉmuṟai yāṉum parantaṉa varūum
uricco lellām poruṭkuṟai kūṭṭal

varamputamak kiṉmaiyiṉ vaḻinaṉi kaṭaipiṭit
tōmpaṭai yāṇaiyiṟ kiḷantavaṟ ṟiyalāṟ

	(tol-col-uri : 98)

		aṉṉa piṟavuṅ kiḷanta valla

eṉpatu, iṟantatu kāttalām; eṉṉai? icaiyuṅ kuṟippum paṇpumēyaṉṟic cīrttiyum puṉiṟum pōlvaṉa vēṟum uḷa eṭuttōtap paṭṭaṉa eṉavum, eṭuttōtātu icaiyuṅ kuṟippum paṇpumaṉṟic cēṇeṉṟun toṟuveṉṟum varuvaṉa uḷaveṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ.
varamputamak kiṉmaiyiṉ
eṉpatu etirporu ḷuṇarttalum aṟiyātuṭaṉpaṭalumām.
ōmpaṭai yāṇai
eṉpatu āṇai kūṟalām. iṅṅaṉam oru cūttirattuṭ pala vantavaḻi oṉṟē uttiyeṉṟuṇarātu maṉattiṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇṭu ivvāṟṟāṉ iṉattiṟ cērttuka eṉṟāṉ āciriyaṉeṉpatu.
		marapunilai tiriyā māṭciya vāki
yuraipaṭu nūṟā miruvakai nilaiya	(tol-mara. 93)
eṉpatu mutalāka ittuṇaiyum vaḻakku nūlilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṉāṉ. eḻunilatteḻunta ceyyuḷiṉ (476) ituvum oṉṟākaliṉ itaṉaic ceyyuḷiyaliṟ kūṟiṉāṉ, īṇṭu marapu kūṟum vaḻi oḻinta ceyyuṭkup pōlātu, nūliṟkup poruṭpaṭai (yākiya yāppuk kuṟṟaṅkaḷum uttivakaiyuṅ kūṟal vēṇṭutaliṟ kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟākkāṟ pāṭṭiṉ marapu kaṭṭurai pōlavuṅ
kaṭṭaḷai araṅkiṉṟi vaṭṭā ṭiyatu*
pōlavum varampiṉṟi vēṇṭiyavāṟu nūl ceytal vilakkiṉṟā vāṉ cellum; cellavē, eḻucīrāṉākiya muṭukiyalaṭiyāṉum ettuṇaiyum nīṇṭatoru vañcippāṭṭāṉum piṟavum vēṇṭiyavāṟuñ cūttirañ ceytalum, oruvaṉ peyariṉai avvac cūttirac ceyyuḷuṭ cārttuvakaiyāṟ peytu kūṟalum, uṇarttappaṭum poruḷiṉai mutaṉūluṭ kiṭantavāṟu pōlātu marapu nilai tiriyac ceytalum, nālvakai yāppoṭu māṟupaṭac ceytalum, vēṇṭiyavar vēṇṭiyavāṟṟāṟ cila ceytalum, vēṇṭiyavar vēṇṭiyavāṟṟāṟ cila poruḷkaḷai vēṟu tōṟṟikkoṇṭu nūl ceytalum vilakkiṉṟākal paṭumeṉpatu.
iṉi uttivakaiyum avvāṟē iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉaveṉap paṭum. eṉṉai? uṇarttappaṭum poruḷ ituveṉṟu aṟivittalum, eḻuttuc coṟporuḷeṉap pakuttuk koṇṭu atikārañ ceytalum, uṇarvu pulaṅkoḷḷumāṟṟāl tokuttuk kāṭṭalum, maṟṟu avaṟṟai vakuttuk kāṭṭiya vaḻip payamil kūṟaleṉṟu karutāmal atu taṉṉāṉoru payampaṭac ceytalum, mutaṉūlāyiṉa vellām nūṟ poruḷuṇartaṟkuk karuviyā kaluñ cūttiraccurukkattuk kētuvākalum uṭaimaiyiṉ avaiyum vēṇṭappaṭṭaṉa veṉpatu.
iṉi, avaṟṟai ittuṇaiyeṉa varaiyaṟākkāl et tuṇaiyum palavāki ikantōṭutalum, vaḻinūṉ mutaṉūl vaḻittaṉṟākalum paṭum. muppattu mūṉṟeḻut teṉṟāṉāyiṉum appeṟṟittaṉṟi aṟupattāṟākak koḷḷavaittāṉeṉṟu uttikūṟutalum uṭampaṭuvāṉātal cellumeṉa maṟukka.
iṉi,
nuṉittaku pulavar kūṟiya nūl
eṉṟataṉāṉē; pāyiracceyyuḷuñ cūttiracceyyuḷum āciriyappāvum veṇpāvum peṟṟu varutalum, av virupāvum peṟṟu varutalum, pāṭṭuppōla ellāvuṟuppum peṟutaṟkuc cellā veṉpatūum, māttirai mutalākap pāvīṟāka vanta patiṉoṉṟum vaṇṇaṅkaḷuḷ ēṟpaṉa koḷḷiṉuṅ koḷḷumeṉavum yāppuṟuppuk koḷḷuṅkāl īṇṭotiya marapuṅkoḷḷappaṭumeṉavuṅ koḷka. uraikkuṅ kāṇṭikaikkum ivaṟṟuḷḷum ēṟpaṉa aṟintu koḷka.
iṉṉum nuṉittaku pulavar eṉṟataṉāṉē * tantiramuñ cūttiramum viruttiyumeṉa mūṉṟum oruvarēyaṉṟi oṉṟu oruvar ceytalum, iraṇṭu ceytalum peṟappaṭumeṉṟalum, orucālai māṇākkarun tammiṭai nūl kēṭṭa māṇākkarum pāyirañ ceyyappeṟupa veṉṟalum, potuppāyiramuñ ciṟappup pāyiramumeṉa appāyirantām iraṇṭāmeṉṟalum, īvōṉṟaṉmaiyum ītaliyaṟkaiyuṅ koḷvōṉṟaṉ maiyuṅ kōṭaṉmarapumeṉṟa nāṉkuṟuppuṭaiyatu potup pāyirameṉṟalum, ataṉvaḻiyē kūṟappaṭuñ ciṟappuppāyiran tāṉ eṭṭilakkaṇa muṭaitteṉṟalum, avai ākkiyōṉ peyarum vaḻiyum ellaiyum nūṟpeyarum yāppum nutaliya poruḷuṅ kēṭpōrum payaṉu (naṉṉūl : 47) meṉappaṭu meṉṟalum, avai tām nūṟkiṉṟiyamaiyāveṉak koḷḷap paṭutaluṅ kūṟi muṭikka.
ciṟappuppāyirattāṉē nūlilakkaṇam oru vakaiyāṉ uṇarappaṭum; potuppāyirattāṉē āciriyarum māṇākkarum nūluraittalum nūlkēṭṭalum mācaṟavaṟintu urainaṭāttu vārāka ataṉāṉē ivai nūṉmukattiṉiṉṟu nilāvu meṉpatu.

āyvurai :
ilakkaṇa nūlāciriyaṉ tāṉkūṟa  eṭuttukkoṇṭa poruḷaic cūttirattāṟ pulappaṭutta mēṟkoṇṭuuṇarttum muṟaimaiyē utti eṉappaṭum, itu tantiravutti eṉa aṭai puṇarttu vaḻaṅkappeṟum. tantiram---nūl. utti---yutti. tantira meṉiṉum nūleṉiṉum okkum. uttiyeṉpatu vaṭamoḻic citaivu. atu cūttirattiṟkiṭappatōr poruḷ vēṟupāṭu kāṭṭuvatu eṉa viḷakkuvar iḷampūraṇar. uttiyiṉai nūṟ puṇarppu eṉa vaḻaṅkutalum uṇṭu. utti iṉṉa teṉpataṉai viḷakkum muṟaiyil amaintatu.

eṉavaruvatu tolkāppiya marapiyaliṉ iṟutic cūttiramākum. itaṟku iḷampūraṇarum pērāciriyarum cila viṭaṅkaḷil vev vēṟu pāṭaṅkoṇṭu urai varaintuḷḷaṉar.
iccūttirattil otta kāṭci utti eṉa aṭai koṭut tōtiṉamaiyāl muṉṉaic cūttirattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa pattuvakaik kuṟṟattōṭum ottu nōkkum nilaiyatu utti eṉavum utti eṉṟamaiyātu uttivakai eṉat terittuṇarttiṉa maiyāl nūlāciriyaṉ nuṇṇuṇarviṉ tiṟattāl iyalpāka amaiyum nūṟpuṇarppu muṟaiyē utti eṉappaṭum eṉavum, in nūṟpāvil nutaliyataṟital mutalāka eṭutturaikkappaṭuvaṉa nūlāciriyaṉ taṉ karuttuppaṭi ceyaṟkaiyāka amaittuk koḷḷum muṟaimaiyātaliṉ uttivakai eṉappaṭṭa eṉavum, uttivakaivirippiṉ eṉavē iṅkuk kūṟappaṭum uttivakai ovvoṉṟiṉum iṉamāka aṭakkappaṭuvaṉa uḷa eṉavum koḷvar pērāciriyar,
pattu vakaik kuṟṟattōṭum ottuvarum eṉavē ivai nūṟkaḷ aṉṟi oḻinta ceyyuṭku varuṅkāl vilakkappaṭutalum, muṟkūṟiya kuṟṟampōla ivaiyum vēṟucila poruḷ paṭaittaluṭaiyavāyiṉa. kāṭci yutti eṉṟu ivaṟṟaik kūṟiyavataṉāṉ nūluṭ kāṇappaṭum. aintu kuṟṟattōṭum (kūṟiyatu kūṟal, māṟukoḷak kūṟal, mikaippaṭak kūṟal. (poruḷil moḻital, mayaṅkak kūṟal eṉṉum aintum maṟṟoruporuḷ koḷḷiṉ avai vacaiyaṟṟaṉa vātal pōla avvannūṟkup payaṉpaṭavarum pakutiyāṉ) ottal koḷḷappaṭum eṉpar pērāciriyar. uktiyeṉpatu ceyaṟkai vakaiyākiya nūṟ puṇarppāyiṉ nūl ceyyuṅkāl iyalpu vakaiyākiya vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum pōlac collutal oṇmaiyuṭaiyataṉṟu eṉiṉ, avvāṟu cevvaṉam (iyalpu vakaiyāṟ) collutal nuṇmaiyoṭu puṇarnta oṇmait tātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu (āciriyaṉ) muṉṉarc colliṉāṉām. eṉavē aṅṅaṉam oṇmaiyuṭaiyatātalē utti eṉpatu tāṉē viḷaṅkum. cevvaṉañ collāta tantiravutti vakaiyum avvāṟē oṇmaiyuṭaiyavām eṉpatu iccūttirattiṉ karuttākum. eṉavē cevvaṉañ ceytalē utti eṉavum nutaliyataṟital mutalāka iṅkuc collappaṭṭavai utti vakaiyeṉavum koḷvar pērāciriyar.
muṉṉar (marapiyal-98) etiratu nōkki muppattiru vakaiyutti eṉat tokai kūṟip piṉṉar (iccūttirattil)
		maṉatti ṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintu koṇ
ṭiṉattiṟ cērttiyuṇarttal vēṇṭum
eṉkiṉṟārākalāṉ aṅṅaṉam iṉampaṟṟi avaṟṟōṭu aṭaṅkuvaṉavellām avaṟṟuviriyākum eṉṉuṅ karuttiṉāl utti vakai virippiṉ eṉṟār āciriyar.
avaṉiva ṉuvaṉeṉa varūum peyarum (col-164) eṉpataṉuḷ, tokaiyiṉṟiyuñ curuṅkac ceyvatōr āṟu (upāyam) uḷatāyiṉum avai patiṉaintu peyarumē oru nikaraṉaveṉpatu aṟiyalākum vaṇṇam appatiṉaintum eṉat tokai kūṟutal veḷḷitaṉṟi oḷḷitākavē yamaitaliṉ tokuttuk kūṟal uttivakaiyāṉ amaiyum eṉavum, iṉi meypeṟu marapiṟṟoṭaivakai tāmē (cey-101) eṉṉuñ cūttiram pōlvaṉa piṟitoru poruḷ payakkumeṉṟalum accūttirattuṭ kāṭṭappaṭṭatu eṉavum ivvāṟṟāl ivvutti vakai ellā nūṟkum iṉṟiyamaiyāvāyiṉa eṉavum kūṟuvar pērāciriyar.
iyaṟkaip poruḷai yiṟṟeṉak kiḷattal (col-19) eṉṟāṟ pōlac collic celvaṉa cevvaṉañ ceyyum uttiyākaliṉ avai uttivakaiyeṉappaṭā uttiyeṉa vēpaṭum eṉpatu pērāciriyar karuttākum. iccūttirattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa muppattiraṇṭu uttivakai ivaiyeṉavum avvakaikku iṉamāka virittuk koḷḷappaṭuvaṉa ivai yeṉavum pērāciriyar utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭi viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
eṇṇiya muppatiraṇṭumallaṉa tōṟṟiṉum avaṟṟuḷ aṭakki avaitām oruṅkuvariṉum vēṟu terintu nūl colliya alla piṟavarumāṟum avai colliya vakaiyāṟ curuṅkanāṭi iṉattiṟ cērttumāṟum pērāciriyaruraiyiṟ kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
ivvuttivakai paṟṟi iḷampūraṇarum pērāciriyarum koṇṭa pāṭaṅkaḷ uttivakaiyiṉ peyarkaḷiṟ cila māṟṟattaik kuṟippaṉavākum.
moḻinta poruḷōṭoṉṟa vaittal, moḻiyāta taṉai muṭṭiṉṟi muṭittal eṉa iḷampūraṇar koṇṭa iraṇṭuttikaḷaiyum moḻinta poruḷoṭoṉṟa av vayiṉ moḻiyātataṉai muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittal eṉa orē uttiyākak koṇṭār pērāciriyar.
vantatu koṇṭu vārātatu muṭittal eṉa iḷam pūraṇarum vārātatuṇarttal eṉap pērāciriyarum pāṭaṅ koṇṭaṉar.
taṉkōṭ kūṟal uṭampoṭu puṇarttal
eṉa iḷampūraṇarum
taṉkōṭkūṟal muṟaipiḻaimai eṉap pērāciriyarum
pāṭaṅ koṇṭaṉar.
orutalaiyaṉmai muṭintatu kāṭṭal eṉpataṉai iḷam pūraṇar orē uttiyākak koṇṭār, pērāciriyar orutalai yaṉmai eṉavum muṭintatukāṭṭal eṉavum iraṇṭākak koṇṭār,
piṟaṉuṭampaṭṭatu tāṉuṭampaṭutal eṉpataṟkup piṟaṉūṉ muṭintatu tāṉuṭampaṭutal eṉavum mūla pāṭam kāṇappaṭutalāl, piṟanūṉ muṭintatu tāṉuṭampaṭutal eṉa yāpparuṅkala viruttiyiṉum naṉṉūl mayilai nātaruraiyilum kāṇappaṭum uttiyum ituvum oṉṟēyeṉpatu naṉku teḷiyap paṭum.
moḻiyātataṉai muṭṭiṉṟi muṭittalāvatu, eṭut tōtāta poruḷai muṭṭuppaṭāmal uraiyiṉāṉ muṭittal. itaṉai uraiyiṟkōṭal eṉpa eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
vārātataṉāṉ vantatu muṭittal vantatu koṇṭu vārātatu muṭittal eṉṉum uttikaḷ collamaippil naṉṉūlil iṭam peṟavillai. naṉṉūlil varum oṉṟiṉa muṭittal taṉṉiṉa muṭittal eṉpatu ōrutti itaṉai iraṇṭuttikaḷākak koṇṭu uyttuṇaravaippu eṉpataṉai uttikaṭkellām aṭaimoḻiyākkutalum uṇṭu. vārātatu koṇṭu vantatu muṭittalai oṉṟiṉa muṭittal eṉavum, vantatu koṇṭu vārātatu muṭittalait taṉṉiṉa muṭittal eṉavum koḷḷutaṟkum iṭamuṇṭu.
muntumoḻintataṉ talai taṭumāṟṟam eṉpataṉait talaitaṭumāṟṟan tantu puṇarnturaittal eṉpatōr utti eṉpar kārikai yuraiyāciriyar.
oppakkūṟal eṉpatu oppiṉ muṭittal māṭṭeṟin toḻukal eṉa naṉṉūlil varum uttikaḷōṭu collālum poruḷālum ottuḷa oṉṟu kūṟuṅkāl itu poruṭ kuṟitta teṉṟu iraṭṭuṟac ceytal, eṉap pērāciriyar viḷakkutalāl iḵtu iraṭṭuṟa moḻital eṉṉum utti eṉpatu avar karutteṉat terikiṟatu.
orutalaimoḻiyāvatu ēkākkarameṉṉum vaṭamoḻip poruṇmai. aḵtāvatu cūttirattukkup poruḷ kavarttut tōṉṟiṉ ataṉuḷ oṉṟiṉait tuṇantu kūṟal eṉpar. iḷam pūraṇar. oru talai moḻital eṉpataṉai naṉṉūlil varum orutalai tuṇital eṉṉum uttiyākak koḷḷalām. taṉ kōṭ kūṟal uṭampoṭupuṇarttal eṉpatu iḷampūraṇar koṇṭa pāṭam. naṉṉūlilum ivvāṟē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. taṉkōṭ kūṟal muṟaipiṟaḻāmai eṉap pāṭaṅ koḷvar pērāciriyar.
14,  15.	iṟantatu kāttal etiratu pōṟṟal eṉṉum iraṇṭum yāpparuṅkalaviruttiyilum naṉṉūlilum iṟantatu vilakkal  etiratu pōṟṟal eṉa āḷappeṟṟaṉa. kāttal eṉṟatu kuṟṟamē kākka (tirukkuṟaḷ-434) eṉpuḻippōla vilakkutal eṉṟa poruḷil āḷappeṟṟatu.
16.	moḻivām eṉṟal eṉpatu ivvirunūlkaḷilum uraittum eṉṟal eṉak kuṟikkap peṟṟatu.
17.	kūṟiṟṟeṉṟal eṉpatu, naṉṉūlil uraittām eṉṟal eṉak kuṟikkappeṟṟatu.
18.	tāṉ kuṟiyiṭutal eṉpatu yāpparuṅkalaviruttiyilum naṉṉūlilum taṉ kuṟi vaḻakkam mikaveṭutturaittal eṉak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
19.	orutalaiyaṉmai muṭintatu kāṭṭal eṉpataṉai ōruttiyākak koṇṭu oruporuḷaiyōtiyavaḻic collu taṟkē yurittaṉṟip piṟa poruṭkum potuvāka muṭittamai kāṭṭal eṉa viḷakkam tantār iḷampūraṇar. 19,20.  oru talaiyaṉmai eṉpatu iraṇṭu moḻital eṉavum muṭintatu kāṭṭal eṉpatu muṭiviṭaṅkūṟal eṉavum yāpparuṅkala viruttiyilum naṉṉūlilum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa uttikaḷākak koḷḷalāmā eṉpatu āyvukkuriyatākum.
21.	āṇai kūṟal eṉpatu yāpparuṅkala viruttiyilum naṉṉūlilum kāṇappaṭvillai.
22.	palporuṭkēṟpiṉ nallatu kōṭal itaṉai ēṟpuḻikkōṭal eṉavum orupuṭaic cēṟal eṉavum collupa eṉpar pērāciriyar.
eṭutta moḻiyiṉ eytavaittal
eṉṟunaṉṉūlil varum utti iṅkuk karutattakkatu.
23.	tokutta moḻiyāṉ vakuttaṉar kōṭal
colliṉ muṭiviṉ apporuḷ muṭittal
	(naṉṉūl)
eṉpatum atu eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
24.	maṟutalai citaittut taṉṟuṇipuraittal eṉpatu yāpparuṅkalaviruttiyilum naṉṉūlilum iṭampeṟavillai.
25, 26.	piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal eṉpataṉaiyum aṟiyātuṭam paṭal eṉpataṉaiyum yāpparuṅkalaviruttiyum naṉṉūluraiyum piṟaṉūṉ muṭintatu tāṉuṭampaṭutal eṉṟa collamaippilēyē aṭakkiyiruttal kūṭum.
27.	poruḷiṭaiyiṭutal eṉpatu oru poruḷai yōtiya vaḻi, ataṟkiṉamākiya poruḷaic cērak kūṟātu iṭaiyīṭupaṭak kūṟutal eṉpar iḷampūraṇar. naṉṉūliluḷḷa muṉmoḻintu kōṭal piṉṉatu niṟuttal eṉṉum iraṇṭu uttikaḷum itaṉkaṇ aṭaṅkum.
28.	etirporuḷuṇarttal eṉpatu naṉṉūlil varum etiratu pōṟṟal eṉṟa uttiyilō allatu muṭiviṭaṅ kūṟal eṉṟa uttiyilō aṭaṅkum.
29.	colliṉeccam colliyāṅkuṇarttal eṉpatu yāpparuṅkalaviruttiyilum naṉṉūlilum varum eñciya colliṉ eytak kūṟal eṉṉum uttiyātal kūṭum.
30.	tantupuṇarn turaittal eṉpatu, naṉṉūlil varum ētuviṉ muṭittal eṉṉum uttiyātal kūṭum.
31.	ñāpakaṅkūṟalāvatu, iraṇṭuṟamoḻintu iraṇṭu coṟkaḷum poruḷkōṭal eṉpatu iḷampūraṇar. cūttiram ceyyuṅkāl aritum peritumākac ceytu maṟṟum ataṉālē vēṟu pala poruḷuṇarttal eṉpar pērāciriyar.
32.	uyttuk koṇṭuṇartal --- iḵtu uytturai vaippu eṉa yāpparuṅkala viruttiyilum naṉṉūlilum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu.
